// FUNCIÓN: Asignar 2 uniformes aleatorios diferentes
function assignRandomUniforms() {
    try {
        if (uniformes.length < 2) return;
        
        // Seleccionar 2 uniformes diferentes
        var idx1 = Math.floor(Math.random() * uniformes.length);
        var idx2 = idx1;
        while (idx2 === idx1) {
            idx2 = Math.floor(Math.random() * uniformes.length);
        }
        
        var uni1 = uniformes[idx1];
        var uni2 = uniformes[idx2];
        
        // Aplicar a los equipos
        room.setTeamColors(Team.RED, uni1.angle, uni1.color, uni1.stripe);
        room.setTeamColors(Team.BLUE, uni2.angle, uni2.color, uni2.stripe);
        
        // almacenar nombres actuales para anuncios posteriores
        currentUniforms.red = uni1.name;
        currentUniforms.blue = uni2.name;
        
        // el aviso de uniformes ahora se muestra en el bloque de arranque de partida
        // room.sendAnnouncement("🏁 Uniformes asignados: 🔴 " + uni1.name + " - 🔵 " + uni2.name, null, 0xFDC43A, "bold");
    } catch (e) {
        console.log("Error asignando uniformes aleatorios: " + e);
    }
}

/* FUNCIONES DE JUEGO */

function checkTime() {
    const scores = room.getScores();
    game.scores = scores;
    if (Math.abs(scores.time - scores.timeLimit) <= 0.01 && scores.timeLimit != 0) {
        if (scores.red != scores.blue) {
            if (checkTimeVariable == false) {
                checkTimeVariable = true;
                setTimeout(() => {
                    checkTimeVariable = false;
                }, 3000);
                scores.red > scores.blue ? endGame(Team.RED) : endGame(Team.BLUE);
                setTimeout(() => {
                    room.stopGame();
                }, 2000);
            }
            return;
        }
        goldenGoal = true;
       // room.sendAnnouncement("⚽ Gol de Oro!", null, 0xF1AF09);
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("TIEMPO EXTRA"), null, Cor.Amarelo, "bold");
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("Se dara " + drawTimeLimit * 120 + " segundos!"), null, Cor.White, "normal");
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("⚽ GOL DE ORO! ⚽"), null, Cor.White, "normal");
    }
    if (scores.time > scores.timeLimit + drawTimeLimit * 120 - 15 && scores.time <= scores.timeLimit + drawTimeLimit * 120) {
        if (checkTimeVariable == false && announced == false) {
            checkTimeVariable = true;
            announced = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
                checkTimeVariable = false;
            }, 10);
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("⌛ ¡15 PARA EL EMPATE!"), null, Cor.Amarelo, "bold");
        }
    }
    if (scores.time > (scores.timeLimit + drawTimeLimit * 120)) {
        if (checkTimeVariable == false) {
            checkTimeVariable = true;
            setTimeout(() => { checkTimeVariable = false; }, 10);
            endGame(Team.SPECTATORS);
            room.stopGame();
            goldenGoal = false;
        }
    }
};

function giveStreakReward(winnerTeam, loserTeamWithStreak, streakNumber) {
    // winnerTeam: Team.RED o Team.BLUE (quien rompió la racha)
    // loserTeamWithStreak: Team.RED o Team.BLUE (quien tenía la racha)
    // streakNumber: número de victorias consecutivas que tenían
    
    var rewardPerPlayer = 7; // unicons/coins
    var winnersTeam = winnerTeam == Team.RED ? TeamR : TeamB;
    var losingTeamOnStreak = loserTeamWithStreak == Team.RED ? TeamR : TeamB;
    
    // Dar recompensa a los que rompieron la racha
    for (var i = 0; i < winnersTeam.length; i++) {
        try {
            var authWinner = getAuth(winnersTeam[i]);
            if (authWinner && localStorage.getItem(authWinner)) {
                var statsWinner = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(authWinner));
                statsWinner[Ss.CO] = (Number(statsWinner[Ss.CO]) || 0) + rewardPerPlayer;
                localStorage.setItem(authWinner, JSON.stringify(statsWinner));
                room.sendAnnouncement("💰 " + winnersTeam[i].name + " + " + rewardPerPlayer + " unicons (Racha rota)", winnersTeam[i].id, 0xFFD700, "normal");
            }
        } catch (e) {}
    }
    
    // Dar recompensa a los que tenían la racha (consuelo)
    for (var i = 0; i < losingTeamOnStreak.length; i++) {
        try {
            var authLoser = getAuth(losingTeamOnStreak[i]);
            if (authLoser && localStorage.getItem(authLoser)) {
                var statsLoser = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(authLoser));
                statsLoser[Ss.CO] = (Number(statsLoser[Ss.CO]) || 0) + rewardPerPlayer;
                localStorage.setItem(authLoser, JSON.stringify(statsLoser));
                room.sendAnnouncement("💰 " + losingTeamOnStreak[i].name + " + " + rewardPerPlayer + " unicons (Racha rota)", losingTeamOnStreak[i].id, 0xFFD700, "normal");
            }
        } catch (e) {}
    }
    
    // Anuncios especiales
    var teamRedName = winnerTeam == Team.RED ? "🔴 ROJO" : "🔵 AZUL";
    var teamBlueName = loserTeamWithStreak == Team.RED ? "🔴 ROJO" : "🔵 AZUL";
    
    room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("🎉 ¡¡RACHA ROTA!! 🎉"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
    room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("El equipo " + teamRedName + " rompió la RACHA de " + streakNumber + " WINS del equipo " + teamBlueName), null, 0xFF6B6B, "bold");
    room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("💎 ¡¡RECOMPENZA DADA!! 💎"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
    room.sendAnnouncement("🏆 Todos recibieron 7 unicons como recompensa especial!", null, 0xFFD700, "normal");
}

function celebrateGoal(scorer, team) {
    // Celebración visual cuando se mete un gol - Cambia el tamaño de los jugadores
    var teamColor = team == Team.RED ? "🔴 ROJO" : "🔵 AZUL";
    var playersTeam = team == Team.RED ? TeamR : TeamB;
    var playersOpponent = team == Team.RED ? TeamB : TeamR;
    
    // Aumentar tamaño del goleador
    try {
        room.setPlayerCustomization(scorer.id, 2.5);
        setTimeout(() => {
            room.setPlayerCustomization(scorer.id, 1);
        }, 4000);
    } catch(e) {}
    
    // Aumentar tamaño de los compañeros de equipo
    try {
        for (var i = 0; i < playersTeam.length; i++) {
            if (playersTeam[i].id != scorer.id) {
                room.setPlayerCustomization(playersTeam[i].id, 1.3);
            }
        }
        setTimeout(() => {
            for (var i = 0; i < playersTeam.length; i++) {
                room.setPlayerCustomization(playersTeam[i].id, 1);
            }
        }, 4000);
    } catch(e) {}
    
    // Reducir tamaño del equipo contrario
    try {
        for (var i = 0; i < playersOpponent.length; i++) {
            room.setPlayerCustomization(playersOpponent[i].id, 0.8);
        }
        setTimeout(() => {
            for (var i = 0; i < playersOpponent.length; i++) {
                room.setPlayerCustomization(playersOpponent[i].id, 1);
            }
        }, 4000);
    } catch(e) {}
    
    // Enviar efectos de celebración con anuncios
    room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("✨✨✨ ¡¡GOOOOOOL!! ✨✨✨"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
    setTimeout(() => {
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("⭐ " + scorer.name + " ¡¡ES UN CAMPEÓN!! ⭐"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
    }, 500);
    setTimeout(() => {
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("🎉 ¡¡CELEBRACIÓN DEL EQUIPO " + teamColor + " 🎉"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
    }, 1000);
    setTimeout(() => {
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("🚀 ¡¡INCREÍBLE! 🚀"), null, Cor.Verde, "normal");
    }, 1500);
}

function celebrateVictory(winnerTeam, loserTeam) {
    // Celebración visual cuando un equipo gana - Cambiar tamaño de los jugadores
    var winnerColor = winnerTeam == Team.RED ? "🔴 ROJO" : "🔵 AZUL";
    var loserColor = loserTeam == Team.RED ? "🔴 ROJO" : "🔵 AZUL";
    var playersWinner = winnerTeam == Team.RED ? TeamR : TeamB;
    var playersLoser = loserTeam == Team.RED ? TeamR : TeamB;
    
    // EQUIPO GANADOR: +40% de tamaño (1.4x)
    try {
        for (var i = 0; i < playersWinner.length; i++) {
            room.setPlayerCustomization(playersWinner[i].id, 1.4);
        }
        setTimeout(() => {
            for (var i = 0; i < playersWinner.length; i++) {
                room.setPlayerCustomization(playersWinner[i].id, 1);
            }
        }, 8000);
    } catch(e) {}
    
    // EQUIPO PERDEDOR: -20% de tamaño (0.8x)
    try {
        for (var i = 0; i < playersLoser.length; i++) {
            room.setPlayerCustomization(playersLoser[i].id, 0.8);
        }
        setTimeout(() => {
            for (var i = 0; i < playersLoser.length; i++) {
                room.setPlayerCustomization(playersLoser[i].id, 1);
            }
        }, 8000);
    } catch(e) {}
    
    // Efectos de victoria para el equipo ganador
    setTimeout(() => {
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("🎊🏆 ¡¡VICTORIA DEL EQUIPO " + winnerColor + "!! 🏆🎊"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
    }, 500);
    setTimeout(() => {
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("⭐✨ ¡¡CAMPEONES!! ✨⭐"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
    }, 1200);
    setTimeout(() => {
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("🎯 ¡¡JUEGO ESPECTACULAR!! 🎯"), null, Cor.Verde, "normal");
    }, 1800);
    
    // Mensaje al equipo perdedor
    setTimeout(() => {
        room.sendAnnouncement("💪 Equipo " + loserColor + ", ¡¡sigan así! ¡¡Próxima vez es suya!!", null, Cor.Branco, "normal");
    }, 2400);
}

function endGame(winner) { // maneja el final de un juego: no hay función stopGame dentro
    players.length >= 2 * maxTeamSize - 1 ? activateChooseMode() : null;
    const scores = room.getScores();
    game.scores = scores;
    Rposs = Rposs/(Rposs+Bposs);
    Bposs = 1 - Rposs;
    endGameVariable = true;
    
    // LÓGICA DE RACHA MEJORADA
    var previousWinner = lastWinner;
    var previousStreak = streak;
    var streakBroken = false;
    
    // Detectar si se rompió una racha anterior
    if (winner != previousWinner && previousWinner != Team.SPECTATORS && previousStreak >= 6) {
        streakBroken = true;
        giveStreakReward(winner, previousWinner, previousStreak);
    }
    
    // Actualizar racha
    if (winner == previousWinner && winner != Team.SPECTATORS) {
        streak++;
    } else if (winner != Team.SPECTATORS) {
        streak = 1;
    } else {
        streak = 0;
    }
    
    lastWinner = winner;
    
    // Procesar resultados de apuestas
    if (winner !== Team.SPECTATORS && bettingWindowActive === false) {
        try {
            processBettingResults(winner);
        } catch(e) {}
    }
    
    // ANUNCIOS
    if (winner == Team.RED) {
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("🏆 EQUIPO ROJO GANÓ 🏆"), null, 0xFDC43A, "bold");
    }
    else if (winner == Team.BLUE) {
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("🏆 EQUIPO AZUL GANÓ 🏆"), null, 0xFDC43A, "bold");
    }
    else {
        streak = 0;
        room.sendAnnouncement("💤 Tiempo limite alcanzado");
    }
    
    // MOSTRAR RACHA BONITA ABAJO DEL GANADOR
    room.sendAnnouncement("", null, 0xFFFFFF);
    
    if (winner != Team.SPECTATORS) {
        if (streak == 1) {
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("Primera victoria"), null, 0x73EC59, "normal");
        }
        else if (streak == 2) {
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("⚡ RACHA DE 2 WINS ⚡"), null, 0xFF6B6B, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("El equipo comienza a dominar!"), null, 0xFF6B6B, "normal");
        }
        else if (streak == 3) {
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("🔥 RACHA DE 3 WINS 🔥"), null, 0xFF6B6B, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("¡Es imparable!"), null, 0xFF6B6B, "normal");
        }
        else if (streak == 4) {
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("💪 RACHA DE 4 WINS 💪"), null, 0xFF6B6B, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("¡Una máquina!"), null, 0xFF6B6B, "normal");
        }
        else if (streak == 5) {
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("⭐ RACHA DE 5 WINS ⭐"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("¡CASI INVENCIBLE!"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
        }
        else if (streak == 6) {
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("👑 RACHA MÁGICA 👑"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("6 WINS SEGUIDOS"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("¡PRÁCTICAMENTE INVENCIBLES!"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
        }
        else if (streak > 6) {
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("👑 RACHA ÉPICA 👑"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText(streak + " WINS SEGUIDOS"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("¡¡¡QUEBRANTA RECORDS!!!"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"), null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
        }
    }
    //room.sendAnnouncement("📊 Posicion de pelota: 🔴 " + (Rposs*100).toPrecision(3).toString() + "% | " + (Bposs*100).toPrecision(3).toString() + "% 🔵", null, 0xFDC43A);
    room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("🏆 FIN DE PARTIDA 🏆"), null, Cor.White, "bold");
    room.sendAnnouncement(centerText(" " + scores.red + " - " + scores.blue), null, Cor.White, "normal");
    room.sendAnnouncement(centerText((Rposs * 100).toPrecision(3).toString() + "% | Posesión de balon | " + (Bposs * 100).toPrecision(3).toString() + "% "), null, Cor.White, "normal");
    scores.red == 0 ? (scores.blue == 0 ? room.sendAnnouncement("🥅 " + GKList[0].name + " es un hombre? no, es una barrera! " + GKList[1].name + " no marco un gol. ", null, 0xFDC43A) : room.sendAnnouncement("🥅 es un hombre? no, es una barrera! " + GKList[1].name + " No marco un solo gol ", null, 0xFDC43A)) : scores.blue == 0 ? room.sendAnnouncement("🥅 es un hombre? no, es una barrera! " + GKList[0].name + " No marco ningun gol ", null, 0xFDC43A) : null;
    updateStats();
    
    // TOP 3 se muestra automáticamente cada 20 minutos (no al final de partida)
    //sendDiscordWebhook(scores);
    //room.sendAnnouncement("La partida fue grabada y enviada en nuestro Discord. ID: " + `${getDate()}`, null, Cor.Amrelo, Negrito);
    
}

function quickRestart() {
    room.stopGame();
    setTimeout(() => {
        room.startGame();
    }, 2000);
}

function resumeGame() {
    assignRandomUniforms(); // Asignar uniformes aleatorios
    setTimeout(() => {
        room.startGame();
    }, 2000);
    setTimeout(() => {
        room.pauseGame(false);
    }, 1000);
}

function activateChooseMode() {
    inChooseMode = true;
    slowMode = 2;
    room.sendAnnouncement("Modo de reclutamiento activado!", null, 0x55bae2, "normal");
}

function deactivateChooseMode() {
    inChooseMode = false;
    clearTimeout(timeOutCap);
    if (slowMode != 0) {
        slowMode = 0;
        room.sendAnnouncement("Modo de reclutamiento cerrado.", null, 0xf2a000, "normal");
    }
    redCaptainChoice = "";
    blueCaptainChoice = "";
}

function loadMap(map, scoreLim, timeLim) {
    if (map != '') {
        room.setCustomStadium(map);
    } else {
        console.log("dio un error cargando el estadio")
        room.setDefaultStadium("Classic");
    }
    room.setScoreLimit(scoreLim);
    room.setTimeLimit(timeLim);
}

/* FUNCIONES DEL JUGADOR */

var _rankAllowed = true; // estado para evitar spam: true = se permiten rangos (>=8 jugadores)

function updateTeams() { // Actualiza la lista de jugadores y la lista de todos los equipos.
    players = room.getPlayerList().filter((player) => player.id != 0 && !getAFK(player));
    TeamR = players.filter(p => p.team === Team.RED);
    TeamB = players.filter(p => p.team === Team.BLUE);
    teamS = players.filter(p => p.team === Team.SPECTATORS);
    // Comportamiento de ranks: si hay menos de 8 jugadores, bloquear subida y avisar una vez
    try {
        if (players.length < 2 * maxTeamSize) {
            if (_rankAllowed) {
                room.sendAnnouncement("Faltan jugadores para poder subir de rank!!", null, 0xFF0000);
                _rankAllowed = false;
            }
        } else {
            // volvieron a 8 o más
            _rankAllowed = true;
        }
    } catch (e) {}
    // Ordenar la fila de espera por marca de skip (skip_<auth>) para que los que usaron !skip vayan al frente
    try {
        teamS.sort((a, b) => {
            var aAuth = getAuth(a);
            var bAuth = getAuth(b);
            var aSkipRaw = aAuth ? localStorage.getItem('skip_' + aAuth) : null;
            var aSkip = aSkipRaw ? Number(aSkipRaw) : 0;
            var bSkipRaw = bAuth ? localStorage.getItem('skip_' + bAuth) : null;
            var bSkip = bSkipRaw ? Number(bSkipRaw) : 0;
            return bSkip - aSkip;
        });
    } catch (e) {}
}

function handleInactivity() { // maneja la inactividad: los jugadores serán expulsados ​​después de afkLimit
    if (countAFK && (TeamR.length + TeamB.length) > 1) {
        for (var i = 0; i < TeamR.length; i++) {
            setActivity(TeamR[i], getActivity(TeamR[i]) + 1);
        }
        for (var i = 0; i < TeamB.length; i++) {
            setActivity(TeamB[i], getActivity(TeamB[i]) + 1);
        }
    }
    for (var i = 0; i < extendedP.length; i++) {
        if (extendedP[i][eP.ACT] == 60 * (4 / 5 * afkLimit)) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("@" + room.getPlayer(extendedP[i][eP.ID]).name + ", si no te mueves en los próximos " + Math.floor(afkLimit / 5) + " segundos, seras kickeado!", extendedP[i][eP.ID], 0xf4a404, "bold", 4);
        }
        if (extendedP[i][eP.ACT] >= 60 * afkLimit) {
            extendedP[i][eP.ACT] = 0;
            if (room.getScores().time <= afkLimit - 0.5) {
                setTimeout(() => {
                    !inChooseMode ? quickRestart() : room.stopGame();
                }, 10);
            }
            room.kickPlayer(extendedP[i][eP.ID], "AFK", false);
        }
    }
}

function getAuth(player) {
    return extendedP.filter((a) => a[0] == player.id) != null ? extendedP.filter((a) => a[0] == player.id)[0][eP.AUTH] : null;
}

// Función para determinar cuántos coins gana al meter gol por rango/VIP
function getGoalCoins(stats) {
    if (!stats) return 1;
    var role = stats[Ss.RL];
    var vipType = stats[Ss.VT];
    var vipExp = stats[Ss.VE];
    
    // Verificar si VIP expiró
    if (vipExp && vipExp < Date.now()) {
        stats[Ss.VT] = null;
        stats[Ss.VE] = 0;
        vipType = null;
    }
    
    if (vipType === "supreme") return 5;    // buff para supreme
    if (vipType === "start") return 3;     // owner-granted start tier
    if (vipType === "vip") return 2;       // tienda
    if (vipType === "medium") return 2;    // medium tiene diferente relación
    if (role === "admin" || role === "mod") return 2;
    if (role === "owner") return 2;
    return 1; // default para jugadores normales
}

// Función para determinar cuántos coins gana al ganar por rango/VIP
function getWinCoins(stats) {
    if (!stats) return 2;
    var role = stats[Ss.RL];
    var vipType = stats[Ss.VT];
    var vipExp = stats[Ss.VE];
    
    // Verificar si VIP expiró (no borrar SA)
    if (vipExp && vipExp < Date.now()) {
        stats[Ss.VT] = null;
        stats[Ss.VE] = 0;
        vipType = null;
    }
    
    if (vipType === "supreme") return 6;  // mayor buff suprem
    if (vipType === "start") return 4;   // owner-granted start
    if (vipType === "medium") return 4;  // medium también fuerte pero distinto
    if (vipType === "vip") return 3;     // tienda
    if (role === "admin" || role === "mod") return 3;
    if (role === "owner") return 3;
    return 2; // default para jugadores normales
}

// Función para obtener stats de forma consistente y segura
function getPlayerStats(player) {
    var auth = getAuth(player);
    if (!auth) return [0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", 0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", "player", player.name, 0, null, 0, "", 0, "", ""];
    
    var stored = localStorage.getItem(auth);
    if (!stored) {
        var defaultStats = [0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", 0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", "player", player.name, 0, null, 0, "", 0, "", ""];
        localStorage.setItem(auth, JSON.stringify(defaultStats));
        return defaultStats;
    }
    
    var stats = JSON.parse(stored);
    // Asegurar que el array tenga al menos 20 elementos (incluye coins, VIP, skip period, bets)
    while (stats.length < 20) {
        stats.push(stats.length === 10 ? "player" : stats.length === 11 ? player.name : stats.length === 13 ? null : stats.length === 15 ? "" : 0);
    }
    return stats;
}

// Enviar lista de Admins y Mods de forma privada a un jugador
function sendAdminModList(toPlayerId) {
    var admins = [];
    var mods = [];
    Object.keys(localStorage).forEach(function(key) {
        if (["player_name", "view_mode", "geo", "avatar", "player_auth_key"].includes(key)) return;
        try {
            var s = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key));
            if (!s) return;
            var role = s[Ss.RL];
            var name = s[Ss.NK] || "";
            if (role == "admin") admins.push({auth: key, name: name});
            if (localStorage.getItem("mod_" + key) || role == "mod") mods.push({auth: key, name: name});
        } catch (e) {}
    });

    room.sendAnnouncement("📋 Lista privada: Administradores y Moderadores", toPlayerId, 0x309D2B, "bold");

    if (admins.length == 0) {
        room.sendAnnouncement("🔐 Admins: ninguno", toPlayerId, 0xFF7900);
    } else {
        var astr = "🔐 Admins: ";
        admins.forEach(function(a) { astr += (a.name ? a.name + " [" + a.auth + "]" : a.auth) + ", "; });
        astr = astr.slice(0, -2);
        room.sendChat(astr, toPlayerId);
    }

    if (mods.length == 0) {
        room.sendAnnouncement("🛡️ Mods: ninguno", toPlayerId, 0x00BFFF);
    } else {
        var mstr = "🛡️ Mods: ";
        mods.forEach(function(m) { mstr += (m.name ? m.name + " [" + m.auth + "]" : m.auth) + ", "; });
        mstr = mstr.slice(0, -2);
        room.sendChat(mstr, toPlayerId);
    }
}

// Función para guardar stats de forma consistente
function setPlayerStats(player, stats) {
    var auth = getAuth(player);
    if (auth) {
        localStorage.setItem(auth, JSON.stringify(stats));
    }
}

function getAFK(player) {
    var found = extendedP.find((a) => a[0] == player.id);
    return found ? found[eP.AFK] : null;
}

function setAFK(player, value) {
    var found = extendedP.find((a) => a[0] == player.id);
    if (found) found[eP.AFK] = value;
}

function getActivity(player) {
    var found = extendedP.find((a) => a[0] == player.id);
    return found ? found[eP.ACT] : null;
}

function setActivity(player, value) {
    var found = extendedP.find((a) => a[0] == player.id);
    if (found) found[eP.ACT] = value;
}

function getGK(player) {
    var found = extendedP.find((a) => a[0] == player.id);
    return found ? found[eP.GK] : null;
}

function setGK(player, value) {
    var found = extendedP.find((a) => a[0] == player.id);
    if (found) found[eP.GK] = value;
}

function getMute(player) {
    var found = extendedP.find((a) => a[0] == player.id);
    return found ? found[eP.MUTE] : null;
}

function setMute(player, value) {
    var found = extendedP.find((a) => a[0] == player.id);
    if (found) found[eP.MUTE] = value;
}

/* FUNCIONES DE BALANCE Y RECLUTAMIENTO */

function updateRoleOnPlayerIn() {
    try {
        updateTeams();
        if (inChooseMode) {
            if (players.length == 6) {
                loadMap(practiceMap, scoreLimitPractice, timeLimitPractice);
            }
            try {
                getSpecList(TeamR.length <= TeamB.length ? TeamR[0] : TeamB[0]);
            } catch (e) { }
        }
        balanceTeams();
    } catch (e) {
        console.log("Error en updateRoleOnPlayerIn: " + e);
    }
}

function updateRoleOnPlayerOut() {
    try {
        updateTeams();
        if (room.getScores() != null) {
            var scores = room.getScores();
            if (players.length >= 2 * maxTeamSize && scores.time >= (5 / 6) * game.scores.timeLimit && TeamR.length != TeamB.length) {
                if (TeamR.length < TeamB.length) {
                    if (scores.blue - scores.red == 2) {
                        endGame(Team.BLUE);
                        room.sendAnnouncement("[𝙵.𝙱.𝙵] Ragequit por parte del equipo rojo detectado, partido terminado!", null, 0xbfff00, "normal");
                        setTimeout(() => {
                            try { room.stopGame(); } catch (e) { }
                        }, 100);
                        return;
                    }
                } else {
                    if (scores.red - scores.blue == 2) {
                        endGame(Team.RED);
                        room.sendAnnouncement("[𝙵.𝙱.𝙵] Ragequit por parte del equipo azul detectado, partido terminado!", null, 0xbfff00, "normal");
                        setTimeout(() => {
                            try { room.stopGame(); } catch (e) { }
                        }, 100);
                        return;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log("Error verificando ragequit: " + e);
    }
    
    try {
        if (inChooseMode) {
            if (players.length < 6) {
                loadMap(practiceMap, scoreLimitPractice, timeLimitPractice);
            }
            if (TeamR.length == 0 || TeamB.length == 0) {
                TeamR.length == 0 ? room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[0].id, Team.RED) : room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[0].id, Team.BLUE);
                return;
            }
            if (Math.abs(TeamR.length - TeamB.length) == teamS.length) {
                room.sendAnnouncement("[𝙵.𝙱.𝙵] Sin posibilidad de reclutamiento, déjame decidir...", null, 0xc0bdb1, "normal");
                deactivateChooseMode();
                resumeGame();
                var b = teamS.length;
                if (TeamR.length > TeamB.length) {
                    for (var i = 0; i < b; i++) {
                        setTimeout(() => {
                            try { room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[0].id, Team.BLUE); } catch (e) { }
                        }, 5 * i);
                    }
                } else {
                    for (var i = 0; i < b; i++) {
                        setTimeout(() => {
                            try { room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[0].id, Team.RED); } catch (e) { }
                        }, 5 * i);
                    }
                }
                return;
            }
            if (streak == 0 && room.getScores() == null) {
                if (Math.abs(TeamR.length - TeamB.length) == 2) {
                    room.sendAnnouncement("Equilibrando equipos...", null, 0xc0bdb1, "normal");
                    TeamR.length > TeamB.length ? room.setPlayerTeam(TeamR[TeamR.length - 1].id, Team.SPECTATORS) : room.setPlayerTeam(TeamB[TeamB.length - 1].id, Team.SPECTATORS);
                }
            }
            if (TeamR.length == TeamB.length && teamS.length < 2) {
                deactivateChooseMode();
                resumeGame();
                return;
            }
            capLeft ? choosePlayer() : getSpecList(TeamR.length <= TeamB.length ? TeamR[0] : TeamB[0]);
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log("Error en balanceo de modo Choose: " + e);
    }
    
    balanceTeams();
}

function balanceTeams() {
    if (!inChooseMode) {
        if (players.length == 1 && TeamR.length == 0) { // 1 jogador
            quickRestart();
            loadMap(practiceMap, scoreLimitPractice, timeLimitPractice);
            room.setPlayerTeam(players[0].id, Team.RED);
        } else if (Math.abs(TeamR.length - TeamB.length) == teamS.length && teamS.length > 0) { // jogadores específicos fornecem jogadores necessários
            const n = Math.abs(TeamR.length - TeamB.length);
            if (TeamR.length > TeamB.length) {
                for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                    room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[i].id, Team.BLUE);
                }
            } else {
                for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                    room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[i].id, Team.RED);
                }
            }
        } else if (Math.abs(TeamR.length - TeamB.length) > teamS.length) { // No hay jugadores suficientes!
            const n = Math.abs(TeamR.length - TeamB.length);
            if (players.length == 1) {
                quickRestart();
                loadMap(practiceMap, scoreLimitPractice, timeLimitPractice);
                room.setPlayerTeam(players[0].id, Team.RED);
                return;
            } else if (players.length == 6) {
                quickRestart();
                loadMap(practiceMap, scoreLimitPractice, timeLimitPractice);
            }
            if (players.length == maxTeamSize * 2 - 1) {
                allReds = [];
                allBlues = [];
            }
            if (TeamR.length > TeamB.length) {
                for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                    room.setPlayerTeam(TeamR[TeamR.length - 1 - i].id, Team.SPECTATORS);
                }
            } else {
                for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                    room.setPlayerTeam(TeamB[TeamB.length - 1 - i].id, Team.SPECTATORS);
                }
            }
        } else if (Math.abs(TeamR.length - TeamB.length) < teamS.length && TeamR.length != TeamB.length) { // modo de reclutamiento
            room.pauseGame(true);
            activateChooseMode();
            choosePlayer();
        } else if (teamS.length >= 2 && TeamR.length == TeamB.length && TeamR.length < maxTeamSize) { // 2 em vermelho, 2 em azul e 2 ou mais especificações
            if (TeamR.length == 2) {
                quickRestart();
                if (!teamS.length == 2) {
                    loadMap(practiceMap, scoreLimitPractice, timeLimitPractice);
                }
            }
            topBtn();
        }
    }
}

function choosePlayer() {
    clearTimeout(timeOutCap);
    if (TeamR.length <= TeamB.length && TeamR.length != 0) {
        room.sendAnnouncement("[Chapulinbot] para elegir a un jugador, poner numero del jugador (4 o 3 dependiendo) o use 'top', 'random' o 'bottom'.", TeamR[0].id, 0x5db5db, "normal");
        timeOutCap = setTimeout(function(player) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("Se rapido, @" + player.name + ", sólo quedan " + Number.parseInt(chooseTime / 2) + " segundos para escoger!", player.id, 0xf2a000, "normal");
            timeOutCap = setTimeout(function(player) {
                room.kickPlayer(player.id, "No llego a escojer a tiempo", false);
            }, chooseTime * 500, TeamR[0]);
        }, chooseTime * 1000, TeamR[0]);
    } else if (TeamB.length < TeamR.length && TeamB.length != 0) {
        room.sendAnnouncement("[Chapulinbot] para elegir a un jugador, poner numero del jugador (4 o 3 dependiendo) o use 'top', 'random' o 'bottom'.", TeamB[0].id, 0x5db5db, "normal");
        timeOutCap = setTimeout(function(player) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("Se rápido, @" + player.name + ", sólo quedan " + Number.parseInt(chooseTime / 2) + " segundos para escoger!", player.id, 0xf2a000, "normal");
            timeOutCap = setTimeout(function(player) {
                room.kickPlayer(player.id, "No llego a escojer a tiempo", false);
            }, chooseTime * 500, TeamB[0]);
        }, chooseTime * 1000, TeamB[0]);
    }
    if (TeamR.length != 0 && TeamB.length != 0) getSpecList(TeamR.length <= TeamB.length ? TeamR[0] : TeamB[0]);
}

function getSpecList(player) {
    var cstm = "[Reclutamiento] Jugadores: ";
    for (var i = 0; i < teamS.length; i++) {
        if (140 - cstm.length < (teamS[i].name + "[" + (i + 1) + "], ").length) {
            room.sendChat(cstm, player.id);
            cstm = "... ";
        }
        cstm += teamS[i].name + "[" + (i + 1) + "], ";
    }
    cstm = cstm.substring(0, cstm.length - 2);
    cstm += ".";
    room.sendAnnouncement(cstm, player.id, 0xebeb09, "normal");
}

/* FUNCIONES DE ESTADISTICAS */

function getLastTouchOfTheBall() {
    const ballPosition = room.getBallPosition();
    updateTeams();
    for (var i = 0; i < players.length; i++) {
        if (players[i].position != null) {
            var distanceToBall = pointDistance(players[i].position, ballPosition);
            if (distanceToBall < triggerDistance) {
                !activePlay ? activePlay = true : null;
                if (lastTeamTouched == players[i].team && lastPlayersTouched[0] != null && lastPlayersTouched[0].id != players[i].id) {
                    lastPlayersTouched[1] = lastPlayersTouched[0];
                    lastPlayersTouched[0] = players[i];
                }
                lastTeamTouched = players[i].team;
            }
        }
    }
}

function getStats() { // Da posesión, velocidad del balón y GK de cada equipo.
    if (activePlay) {
        updateTeams();
        lastTeamTouched == Team.RED ? Rposs++ : Bposs++;
        var ballPosition = room.getBallPosition();
        point[1] = point[0];
        point[0] = ballPosition;
        ballSpeed = (pointDistance(point[0], point[1]) * 60 * 60 * 60) / 15000;
        var k = [-1, Infinity];
        for (var i = 0; i < TeamR.length; i++) {
            if (TeamR[i].position.x < k[1]) {
                k[0] = TeamR[i];
                k[1] = TeamR[i].position.x;
            }
        }
        k[0] != -1 ? setGK(k[0], getGK(k[0]) + 1) : null;
        k = [-1, -Infinity];
        for (var i = 0; i < TeamB.length; i++) {
            if (TeamB[i].position.x > k[1]) {
                k[0] = TeamB[i];
                k[1] = TeamB[i].position.x;
            }
        }
        k[0] != -1 ? setGK(k[0], getGK(k[0]) + 1) : null;
        findGK();
    }
}

function updateStats() {
    // Ensure player lists are current before updating statistics
    try { updateTeams(); } catch (e) {}
    // Requerir exactamente 2*maxTeamSize jugadores para que las wins/rangos se apliquen
    if (!players || players.length < 2 * maxTeamSize) {
        try { room.sendAnnouncement("Faltan jugadores para poder subir de rank!!", null, 0xFF0000); } catch (e) {}
        return;
    }
    if (players.length >= 2 * maxTeamSize && (game.scores.time >= (5 / 6) * game.scores.timeLimit || game.scores.red == game.scores.scoreLimit || game.scores.blue == game.scores.scoreLimit) && allReds.length >= maxTeamSize && allBlues.length >= maxTeamSize) {
        var stats;
        var winnersMsgs = []; // collect subtle win messages for display
        for (var i = 0; i < allReds.length; i++) {
            var authRed = getAuth(allReds[i]);
            var storedRed = authRed ? localStorage.getItem(authRed) : null;
            stats = storedRed ? JSON.parse(storedRed) : [0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", 0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", "player", allReds[i].name, 0, null, 0, "", 0];
            stats[Ss.GA]++;
            if (lastWinner == Team.RED) {
                var prevWins = Number(stats[Ss.WI]) || 0;
                stats[Ss.WI] = prevWins + 1;
                var winCoinsAmount = getWinCoins(stats);
                stats[Ss.CO] = (Number(stats[Ss.CO]) || 0) + winCoinsAmount;
                winnersMsgs.push({ name: allReds[i].name, prev: prevWins });
            } else if (lastWinner == Team.BLUE) {
                stats[Ss.LS]++;
            } else {
                stats[Ss.DR]++;
            }
            stats[Ss.WR] = (100 * stats[Ss.WI] / stats[Ss.GA]).toPrecision(3);
            localStorage.setItem(getAuth(allReds[i]), JSON.stringify(stats));
        }
        for (var i = 0; i < allBlues.length; i++) {
            var authBlue = getAuth(allBlues[i]);
            var storedBlue = authBlue ? localStorage.getItem(authBlue) : null;
            stats = storedBlue ? JSON.parse(storedBlue) : [0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", 0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", "player", allBlues[i].name, 0, null, 0, "", 0];
            stats[Ss.GA]++;
            if (lastWinner == Team.BLUE) {
                var prevWinsB = Number(stats[Ss.WI]) || 0;
                stats[Ss.WI] = prevWinsB + 1;
                var winCoinsAmount = getWinCoins(stats);
                stats[Ss.CO] = (Number(stats[Ss.CO]) || 0) + winCoinsAmount;
                winnersMsgs.push({ name: allBlues[i].name, prev: prevWinsB });
            } else if (lastWinner == Team.RED) {
                stats[Ss.LS]++;
            } else {
                stats[Ss.DR]++;
            }
            stats[Ss.WR] = (100 * stats[Ss.WI] / stats[Ss.GA]).toPrecision(3);
            localStorage.setItem(getAuth(allBlues[i]), JSON.stringify(stats));
        }
        for (var i = 0; i < game.goals.length; i++) {
            if (game.goals[i].striker != null) {
                if ((allBlues.concat(allReds)).findIndex((player) => player.id == game.goals[i].striker.id) != -1) {
                    stats = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(getAuth(game.goals[i].striker)));
                    stats[Ss.GL]++;
                    localStorage.setItem(getAuth(game.goals[i].striker), JSON.stringify(stats));
                }
            }
            if (game.goals[i].assist != null) {
                if ((allBlues.concat(allReds)).findIndex((player) => player.name == game.goals[i].assist.name) != -1) {
                    stats = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(getAuth(game.goals[i].assist)));
                    stats[Ss.AS]++;
                    localStorage.setItem(getAuth(game.goals[i].assist), JSON.stringify(stats));
                }
            }
        }
        if (allReds.findIndex((player) => player.id == GKList[0].id) != -1) {
            stats = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(getAuth(GKList[0])));
            stats[Ss.GK]++;
            game.scores.blue == 0 ? stats[Ss.CS]++ : null;
            stats[Ss.CP] = (100 * stats[Ss.CS] / stats[Ss.GK]).toPrecision(3);
            localStorage.setItem(getAuth(GKList[0]), JSON.stringify(stats));
        }
        if (allBlues.findIndex((player) => player.id == GKList[1].id) != -1) {
            stats = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(getAuth(GKList[1])));
            stats[Ss.GK]++;
            game.scores.red == 0 ? stats[Ss.CS]++ : null;
            stats[Ss.CP] = (100 * stats[Ss.CS] / stats[Ss.GK]).toPrecision(3);
            localStorage.setItem(getAuth(GKList[1]), JSON.stringify(stats));
        }
    }
}

function findGK() {
    var tab = [
        [-1, ""],
        [-1, ""]
    ];
    for (var i = 0; i < extendedP.length; i++) {
        if (room.getPlayer(extendedP[i][eP.ID]) != null && room.getPlayer(extendedP[i][eP.ID]).team == Team.RED) {
            if (tab[0][0] < extendedP[i][eP.GK]) {
                tab[0][0] = extendedP[i][eP.GK];
                tab[0][1] = room.getPlayer(extendedP[i][eP.ID]);
            }
        } else if (room.getPlayer(extendedP[i][eP.ID]) != null && room.getPlayer(extendedP[i][eP.ID]).team == Team.BLUE) {
            if (tab[1][0] < extendedP[i][eP.GK]) {
                tab[1][0] = extendedP[i][eP.GK];
                tab[1][1] = room.getPlayer(extendedP[i][eP.ID]);
            }
        }
    }
    GKList = [tab[0][1], tab[1][1]];
}

setInterval(() => {
    // Optimización: iterar una sola vez sobre localStorage para todas las estadísticas
    var datos = { games: [], wins: [], goals: [], assists: [], cs: [] };
    var ignoredKeys = ["player_name", "view_mode", "geo", "avatar", "player_auth_key"];
    
    Object.keys(localStorage).forEach(function(key) {
        if (!ignoredKeys.includes(key)) {
            try {
                var parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)); // Parse UNA sola vez
                if (parsed && parsed[Ss.NK]) {
                    datos.games.push([parsed[Ss.NK], parsed[Ss.GA]]);
                    datos.wins.push([parsed[Ss.NK], parsed[Ss.WI]]);
                    datos.goals.push([parsed[Ss.NK], parsed[Ss.GL]]);
                    datos.assists.push([parsed[Ss.NK], parsed[Ss.AS]]);
                    datos.cs.push([parsed[Ss.NK], parsed[Ss.CS]]);
                }
            } catch(e) {}
        }
    });
    
    // Mostrar según el modulo
    var stats = [datos.games, datos.wins, datos.goals, datos.assists, datos.cs];
    var labels = ["Partidas Jugadas", "Vitorias", "Goles", "Asistencias", "CS"];
    var idx = statNumber % 5;
    var tableau = stats[idx];
    
    if (tableau.length >= 5) {
        tableau.sort(function(a, b) { return b[1] - a[1]; });
        room.sendChat(labels[idx] + "> #1 " + tableau[0][0] + ": " + tableau[0][1] + " #2 " + tableau[1][0] + ": " + tableau[1][1] + " #3 " + tableau[2][0] + ": " + tableau[2][1] + " #4 " + tableau[3][0] + ": " + tableau[3][1] + " #5 " + tableau[4][0] + ": " + tableau[4][1]);
    }
    statNumber++;
}, statInterval * 60 * 1000);

/* EVENTOS */

/* MOVIMIENTO DEL JUGADOR */

room.onPlayerJoin = function(player) {
    console.log("---------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("[📢] Nick: " + player.name);
    console.log("[📢] Conn: " + player.conn);
    console.log("[📢] Auth: " + player.auth);
    
    try {
        // Verificar si el jugador está baneado
        var banKey = "ban_" + player.auth;
        var banInfo = localStorage.getItem(banKey);
        if (banInfo) {
            try {
                var ban = JSON.parse(banInfo);
                var now = Date.now();
                var timeSinceBan = now - ban.bannedAt;
                
                if (ban.duration === -1 || timeSinceBan < ban.duration) {
                    // Ban aún activo
                    room.kickPlayer(player.id, "🚫 ESTÁS BANEADO del servidor. Razón: " + ban.reason, false);
                    console.log("[🚫 BAN] " + player.name + " (" + player.auth + ") fue rechazado por ban activo");
                    return;
                } else {
                    // Ban expirado, eliminar del storage
                    localStorage.removeItem(banKey);
                }
            } catch (e) { console.log("Error al procesar ban: " + e); }
        }
    } catch (e) { console.log("Error al verificar ban: " + e); }
    
    extendedP.push([player.id, player.auth, player.conn, false, 0, 0, false]);
    // Restaurar estado de mute persistente si existe y no expiró
    try {
        var muteKeyCheck = "mute_" + player.auth;
        if (localStorage.getItem(muteKeyCheck)) {
            try {
                var muteExpiry = Number(localStorage.getItem(muteKeyCheck));
                if (!isNaN(muteExpiry) && muteExpiry > Date.now()) {
                    extendedP[extendedP.length - 1][eP.MUTE] = true;
                    room.sendAnnouncement("🔇 " + player.name + " está MUTEADO (persistente)", player.id, 0xFF7900);
                } else if (!isNaN(muteExpiry) && muteExpiry <= Date.now()) {
                    localStorage.removeItem(muteKeyCheck);
                }
            } catch (e) { }
        }
    } catch (e) { }

    // PROCESAMIENTO PRINCIPAL CON VALIDACIONES: evitar múltiples updateRoleOnPlayerIn()
    var shouldUpdate = false;
    var announceMsg = "";
    var announceColor = 0x5EE7FF;
    var isSpecial = false;

    // Si el AUTH del jugador está en ownersAuths, asignarlo como OWNER automáticamente
    try {
        var hasOwnerAuth = ownersAuths && ownersAuths.includes(player.auth);
        if (hasOwnerAuth) {
            var statsOwner = getPlayerStats(player);
            statsOwner[Ss.RL] = "owner";
            setPlayerStats(player, statsOwner);
            room.setPlayerAdmin(player.id, true);
            announceMsg = "👑 [OWNER] " + player.name + " entró a la sala";
            announceColor = 0xFFD700;
            isSpecial = true;
        }
    } catch (e) { }
    
    // VERIFICAR RANGO TE VERDE (✤Colla♡) - Mensaje especial de entrada
    if (!isSpecial) {
        try {
            var duStats = localStorage.getItem(player.auth) ? JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(player.auth)) : null;
            if (duStats && duStats[Ss.DU]) {
                // Mostrar mensaje especial con colores rosado y verde
                room.sendAnnouncement("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━", null, 0xEA5EC6, "bold");
                room.sendAnnouncement("✨ [✤collita♡] - " + player.name + " ✨", null, 0xEA5EC6, "bold");
                room.sendAnnouncement("Ha entrado al servidor!", null, 0x2B7A5A, "bold");
                room.sendAnnouncement("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━", null, 0x2B7A5A, "bold");
                isSpecial = true;
            }
        } catch (e) { }
    }

    // Verificar rol persistente (admin/mod) SOLO si no es owner
    if (!isSpecial) {
        try {
            var stored = localStorage.getItem(player.auth);
            if (stored) {
                try {
                    var playerRole = JSON.parse(stored)[Ss.RL];
                    if (playerRole == "admin") {
                        room.setPlayerAdmin(player.id, true);
                        announceMsg = "🔐 [ADMIN] " + player.name + " entró a la sala";
                        announceColor = 0xFF7900;
                        isSpecial = true;
                    } else if (playerRole == "mod" || localStorage.getItem("mod_" + player.auth)) {
                        announceMsg = "🛡️ [MOD] " + player.name + " entró a la sala";
                        announceColor = 0x6ECAFF;
                        isSpecial = true;
                    }
                } catch (e) { }
            }
        } catch (e) { }
    }

    // Verificar si está en Top 3 SOLO si no es Admin/Owner
    if (!isSpecial) {
        try {
            var top3 = getTop3Wins();
            var isInTop3 = false;
            var medalEmoji = "";
            var topPosition = -1;
            for (var i = 0; i < top3.length; i++) {
                if (top3[i][0] == player.name) {
                    isInTop3 = true;
                    topPosition = i;
                    if (i == 0) medalEmoji = "🥇";
                    else if (i == 1) medalEmoji = "🥈";
                    else if (i == 2) medalEmoji = "🥉";
                    break;
                }
            }
            
            if (isInTop3) {
                if (topPosition == 0) {
                    announceMsg = medalEmoji + " EL TOP 1 @" + player.name + " ENTRO A LA SALA A SEGUIR VICIANDO";
                } else if (topPosition == 1) {
                    announceMsg = medalEmoji + " EL TOP 2 @" + player.name + " LLEGO... PERO EL TOP 1 LO DOMINA";
                } else if (topPosition == 2) {
                    announceMsg = medalEmoji + " EL TOP 3 @" + player.name + " ENTRA...";
                }
                announceColor = 0xFFD700;
                isSpecial = true;
            }
        } catch (e) { }
    }

    // Verificar si es VIP SOLO si no es especial
    if (!isSpecial) {
        try {
            var vipKey = "vip_" + player.auth;
            var vipData = localStorage.getItem(vipKey) ? JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(vipKey)) : null;
            if (vipData && vipData.type) {
                var vipName = getVipName(vipData.type);
                var vipColor = getVipColor(vipData.type);
                announceMsg = vipName + " " + player.name + " entró a la sala";
                announceColor = vipColor;
                isSpecial = true;
            }
        } catch (e) { }
    }

    // Mostrar anuncio de bienvenida (una sola vez)
    if (isSpecial) {
        room.sendAnnouncement(announceMsg, null, announceColor, "bold");
    } else {
        room.sendAnnouncement("👋🏼 Hola, " + player.name + "!", null, 0x5EE7FF, "bold");
    }
    
    // TOP 3 se muestra automáticamente cada 20 minutos (ver startTop3Timer)
    
    // Mostrar mensaje de bienvenida a TODOS los jugadores que entren
    setTimeout(() => {
        try {
            room.sendAnnouncement(" ---------------------------------------------------", player.id, 0xEDC021, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement("¡Bienvenido a la Liga Boliviana! 🇧🇴", player.id, 0xEDC021, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement("Gracias por jugar con nosotros! 🎮⚽", player.id, 0xEDC021, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(" ---------------------------------------------------", player.id, 0xEDC021, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement("📋 Comandos útiles: !tops (ranking) | !me y !banca | !unis (cambiar uniforme)", player.id, 0x5EE7FF, "normal");
            room.sendAnnouncement("🏆 Gana partidas y sube en el ranking! Suerte!", player.id, 0x159c13, "normal");
        } catch (e) { }
    }, 1500);
    
    // Crear/cargar stats del jugador
    try {
        if (localStorage.getItem(getAuth(player)) == null) {
            var stats = [0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", 0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", "player", player.name, 0, null, 0, "", 0]
            localStorage.setItem(getAuth(player), JSON.stringify(stats));
        }
    } catch (e) { }
    
    // LLAMADA ÚNICA a updateRoleOnPlayerIn al final
    try {
        updateRoleOnPlayerIn();
    } catch (e) { 
        console.log("Error en updateRoleOnPlayerIn: " + e);
    }
}

room.onPlayerTeamChange = function(changedPlayer, byPlayer) {
    try {
        if (changedPlayer.id == 0) {
            room.setPlayerTeam(0, Team.SPECTATORS);
            return;
        }
        if (getAFK(changedPlayer) && changedPlayer.team != Team.SPECTATORS) {
            room.setPlayerTeam(changedPlayer.id, Team.SPECTATORS);
            try { room.sendChat(changedPlayer.name + " está AFK!"); } catch (e) { }
            return;
        }
        updateTeams();
        if (room.getScores() != null) {
            try {
                var scores = room.getScores();
                if (changedPlayer.team != Team.SPECTATORS && scores.time <= (3 / 4) * scores.timeLimit && Math.abs(scores.blue - scores.red) < 2) {
                    (changedPlayer.team == Team.RED) ? allReds.push(changedPlayer): allBlues.push(changedPlayer);
                }
            } catch (e) { }
        }
        if (changedPlayer.team == Team.SPECTATORS) {
            setActivity(changedPlayer, 0);
        }
        
        // Evento crítico: evitar llamadas múltiples a choosePlayer() con delays apropiados
        if (inChooseMode && resettingTeams == false && byPlayer.id == 0) {
            try {
                if (Math.abs(TeamR.length - TeamB.length) == teamS.length) {
                    deactivateChooseMode();
                    resumeGame();
                    var b = teamS.length;
                    if (TeamR.length > TeamB.length) {
                        for (var i = 0; i < b; i++) {
                            setTimeout(() => {
                                try { room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[0].id, Team.BLUE); } catch (e) { }
                            }, 200 * i);
                        }
                    } else {
                        for (var i = 0; i < b; i++) {
                            setTimeout(() => {
                                try { room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[0].id, Team.RED); } catch (e) { }
                            }, 200 * i);
                        }
                    }
                    return;
                } else if ((TeamR.length == maxTeamSize && TeamB.length == maxTeamSize) || (TeamR.length == TeamB.length && teamS.length < 2)) {
                    deactivateChooseMode();
                    resumeGame();
                    return;
                } else if (TeamR.length <= TeamB.length && redCaptainChoice != "") {
                    try {
                        redCaptainChoice == "top" ? room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[0].id, Team.RED) : redCaptainChoice == "random" ? room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[getRandomInt(teamS.length)].id, Team.RED) : room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[teamS.length - 1].id, Team.RED);
                    } catch (e) { }
                    return;
                } else if (TeamB.length < TeamR.length && blueCaptainChoice != "") {
                    try {
                        blueCaptainChoice == "top" ? room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[0].id, Team.BLUE) : blueCaptainChoice == "random" ? room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[getRandomInt(teamS.length)].id, Team.BLUE) : room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[teamS.length - 1].id, Team.BLUE);
                    } catch (e) { }
                    return;
                } else {
                    choosePlayer();
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.log("Error en onPlayerTeamChange (chooseMode): " + e);
            }
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log("Error en onPlayerTeamChange: " + e);
    }
}

room.onPlayerLeave = function(player) {
    try {
        if (TeamR.findIndex((red) => red.id == player.id) == 0 && inChooseMode && TeamR.length <= TeamB.length) {
            choosePlayer();
            capLeft = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
                capLeft = false;
            }, 10);
        }
        if (TeamB.findIndex((blue) => blue.id == player.id) == 0 && inChooseMode && TeamB.length < TeamR.length) {
            choosePlayer();
            capLeft = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
                capLeft = false;
            }, 10);
        }
        setActivity(player, 0);
        updateRoleOnPlayerOut();
    } catch (e) {
        console.log("Error en onPlayerLeave: " + e);
    }
}

room.onPlayerKicked = function(kickedPlayer, reason, ban, byPlayer) {
    ban == true ? banList.push([kickedPlayer.name, kickedPlayer.id]) : null;
}

/* ACTIVIDAD DEL JUGADOR */

// Evento de mensaje en el chat
room.onPlayerChat = function(player, message) {
    // Manejo de emojis de avatar
    var msgEmoji = message.toLowerCase();
    if (msgEmoji.startsWith("!")) {
        msgEmoji = msgEmoji.substring(1);
    }
    if (avatarEmojis[msgEmoji]) {
        changeAvatarTemporarily(player, avatarEmojis[msgEmoji], 3000); // Cambia el avatar por 3 segundos
    }

    // Ensure Owners defined by AUTH keep Owner role and admin flag
    try {
        var _auth_chk = getAuth(player);
        if (ownersAuths && ownersAuths.includes(_auth_chk)) {
            var _sOwner = getPlayerStats(player);
            if (_sOwner[Ss.RL] !== "owner") {
                _sOwner[Ss.RL] = "owner";
                setPlayerStats(player, _sOwner);
            }
            room.setPlayerAdmin(player.id, true);
        }
    } catch (e) {}

    // Mensajes privados con @@nombre mensaje -> envía solo al jugador destino en color verde WhatsApp
    try {
        if (message.startsWith('@@')) {
            var payload = message.slice(2).trim();
            if (!payload) {
                room.sendAnnouncement('Uso: @@nombre mensaje', player.id, 0xFF0000);
                return false;
            }
            var parts = payload.split(' ');
            var targetName = parts[0];
            if (targetName.startsWith('@')) targetName = targetName.slice(1);
            var text = payload.slice(parts[0].length).trim();
            if (!text) {
                room.sendAnnouncement('Escribe un mensaje luego del nombre. Ej: @@juanito hola', player.id, 0xFF0000);
                return false;
            }
            // Buscar jugador por nombre (primero exacto, luego case-insensitive)
            var target = findPlayerByName(targetName);
            if (!target) {
                room.sendAnnouncement('Jugador no encontrado: ' + targetName, player.id, 0xFF0000);
                return false;
            }
            var senderName = player.name;
            var out = 'Whatsapp @' + senderName + ' : ' + text;
            // Color WhatsApp
            var whatsappGreen = 0x25D366;
            room.sendAnnouncement(out, target.id, whatsappGreen, 'normal');
            // Confirmación al emisor
            room.sendAnnouncement('▶ Mensaje privado enviado a @' + target.name + ': ' + text, player.id, whatsappGreen, 'normal');
            return false;
        }
    } catch (e) {}

    // Comando !memide con cooldown de 2 minutos y respuestas aleatorias (varios tamaños)
    try {
        if (message.toLowerCase() === '!memide') {
            // OWNER NO tiene cooldowns
            var memideStats = getPlayerStats(player);
            var isOwnerMemide = memideStats && memideStats[Ss.RL] == "owner";
            
            if (!isOwnerMemide) {
                var authKey = getAuth(player) || player.id;
                var cdKey = 'memide_cd_' + authKey;
                var last = Number(localStorage.getItem(cdKey)) || 0;
                var now = Date.now();
                var cooldown = 2 * 60 * 1000; // 2 minutos
                if (now - last < cooldown) {
                    var wait = Math.ceil((cooldown - (now - last)) / 1000);
                    room.sendAnnouncement('⏳ Espera ' + wait + 's antes de volver a usar !memide', player.id, 0xFF0000);
                    return false;
                }
                localStorage.setItem(cdKey, String(now));
            }
            
            var variants = [
                '26cm UFF TITAN',
                '2cm lo tuyo ya no tiene arreglo JAJAJA',
                '50cm que mierda??? JAJAJA',
                '12cm UF tremenda anaconda',
                '8cm? JAJAJA',
                '4cm falta de sopa JAJAJA',
                '15cm UF tremenda pija bro',
                '30cm UFFF QUE MONSTRUO',
                '0cm JAJAJAJAJA sin comentarios',
            ];
            var pick = variants[Math.floor(Math.random() * variants.length)];
            var whatsappGreen = 0x25D366;
            
            // Obtener stats para mostrar ranking como en !8ball
            var memideStats = getPlayerStats(player);
            var rolePrefix = "";
            if (memideStats[Ss.RL] == "owner") {
                rolePrefix = "[OWNER] ";
            } else if (memideStats[Ss.RL] == "admin") {
                rolePrefix = "[ADMIN] ";
            } else if (memideStats[Ss.RL] == "mod") {
                rolePrefix = "[MOD] ";
            } else if (memideStats[Ss.WI] >= 300) {
                rolePrefix = "[👹] - [🏆: " + memideStats[Ss.WI] + "] Legend ";
            }
            
            // Mostrar quién hizo el comando
            room.sendAnnouncement(rolePrefix + player.name + " pregunta: !memide", null, 0xFFD700, "normal");
            // Mostrar la respuesta de ChapuBot
            room.sendAnnouncement('ChapuBot😳: ' + pick, null, whatsappGreen, 'bold');
            return false;
        }
    } catch (e) {}

    if (message.length > 1 && message[0].toLowerCase() == 't' && message[1] == ' ') {
        if (player.team != 0) {
        room.getPlayerList().forEach((element) => {if (element.team == player.team) room.sendAnnouncement("[CHAT DEL EQUIPO] " + player.name + ": " + message.substr(2), element.id, (player.team == 1 ? 16725591 : 3261685), "bold", 0)} )
        return false
        }
        else {
        room.sendAnnouncement("No estas en un equipo.", player.id)
        }}

    // SELEÇÕES //

    if (message == "!bra") {
        if (player.team == Team.RED) {
            if (player.id == TeamR[0].id) {
                CaptainChoice = "!bra";
                room.setTeamColors(Team.RED, 180, 0x004D0D, [0xD6DB35, 0xD6DB35, 0x007313]);
                room.sendAnnouncement("El capitán del equipo rojo, "+ player.name + ", escogió el uniforme de Brasil!", null, 0x30F55F, "bold");
            }
        }
    }
    if (message == "!bra") {

    try {
        var playerAuth = getAuth(player);
        var currentMessageLower = originalMessage.toLowerCase().trim();
        
        // Inicializar historial del jugador si no existe
        if (!playerSpamMessages[player.id]) {
            playerSpamMessages[player.id] = [];
        }
        
        // Agregar mensaje actual al historial
        playerSpamMessages[player.id].push(currentMessageLower);
        
        // Mantener solo los últimos 7 mensajes en el historial
        if (playerSpamMessages[player.id].length > SPAM_LIMIT) {
            playerSpamMessages[player.id].shift();
        }
        
        // Verificar si los últimos 7 mensajes son idénticos (spam detectado)
        if (playerSpamMessages[player.id].length === SPAM_LIMIT) {
            var esSpam = true;
            var primerMensaje = playerSpamMessages[player.id][0];
            
            for (var i = 1; i < SPAM_LIMIT; i++) {
                if (playerSpamMessages[player.id][i] !== primerMensaje) {
                    esSpam = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
            
            // Si es spam (7 mensajes iguales seguidos)
            if (esSpam) {
                var numAdvertencias = playerSpamWarnings[playerAuth] || 0;
                var timestamp = new Date().toLocaleString();
                
                // Inicializar contador de advertencias si no existe
                if (!playerSpamWarnings[playerAuth]) {
                    playerSpamWarnings[playerAuth] = 0;
                }
                
                // Primera advertencia
                if (numAdvertencias === 0) {
                    playerSpamWarnings[playerAuth] = 1;
                    room.kickPlayer(player.id, "🔇 " + player.name + ": Spam detectado (6 mensajes). Silenciado 10 segundos.", false);
                    room.sendAnnouncement("🔇 " + player.name + ": Spam detectado (6 mensajes). Silenciado 10 segundos.", null, 0xFFAE00, "bold");
                    sendSpamReportToDiscord("warning1", player.name, playerAuth, timestamp);
                    return false;
                }
                
                // Segunda advertencia
                else if (numAdvertencias === 1) {
                    playerSpamWarnings[playerAuth] = 2;
                    room.kickPlayer(player.id, "🔇 " + player.name + ": ADVERTENCIA 2 - Spam persistente. Silenciado 30 segundos.", false);
                    room.sendAnnouncement("🔇 " + player.name + ": ADVERTENCIA 2 - Spam persistente. Silenciado 30 segundos.", null, 0xFF6B35, "bold");
                    sendSpamReportToDiscord("warning2", player.name, playerAuth, timestamp);
                    return false;
                }
                
                // Tercera infracción: BAN AUTOMÁTICO
                else if (numAdvertencias >= 2) {
                    playerSpamWarnings[playerAuth] = 3;
                    room.banPlayer(player.id, "🚫 " + player.name + ": BAN AUTOMÁTICO por spam después de 2 advertencias.", "24h", true);
                    room.sendAnnouncement("🚫 " + player.name + ": BAN AUTOMÁTICO por spam después de 2 advertencias.", null, 0xFF0000, "bold");
                    sendSpamReportToDiscord("ban", player.name, playerAuth, timestamp);
                    return false;
                }
            } else {
                // Si no es spam, limpiar el contador pero mantener advertencias
                playerSpamMessages[player.id] = [currentMessageLower];
            }
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log("Error en sistema anti-spam: " + e);

    else if (["!afk"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        if (players.length != 1 && player.team != Team.SPECTATORS) {
            if (player.team == Team.RED && streak > 0 && room.getScores() == null) {
                room.setPlayerTeam(player.id, Team.SPECTATORS);
            } else {
                room.sendAnnouncement("Usted no puede permanecer AFK mientras está jugando!", player.id, 0xFF7B08);
                return false;
            }
        } else if (players.length == 1 && !getAFK(player)) {
            room.setPlayerTeam(player.id, Team.SPECTATORS);
        }
        setAFK(player, !getAFK(player));
        room.sendAnnouncement(player.name + (getAFK(player) ? " está AFK!" : " Ya no esta mas AFK!"), null, (getAFK(player) ? 0xFF7B08 : 0x8FFF8F));
        getAFK(player) ? updateRoleOnPlayerOut() : updateRoleOnPlayerIn();
        localStorage.getItem(getAuth(player)) ? stats = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(getAuth(player))) : stats = [0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", 0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", "player", player.name, 0, null, 0, "", 0];

            if (getAFK(player) && stats[Ss.RL] != "vip") {
                room.kickPlayer(player.id, "Tempo de afk excedido", false)
            }
        }, 30 * 60 * 1000)
        return false;

    } else if (["!comprarskip"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        var playerStats = getPlayerStats(player);
        var playerCoins = Number(playerStats[Ss.CO]) || 0;
        var role = playerStats[Ss.RL];
        var vipType = playerStats[Ss.VT];
        var vipExp = playerStats[Ss.VE];
        var skipCost = 225;
        
        // Verificar si VIP expiró
        if (vipExp && vipExp < Date.now()) {
            playerStats[Ss.VT] = null;
            playerStats[Ss.VE] = 0;
            vipType = null;
        }
        
        // Determinar límite de skips según rango
        var skipLimit = 2;
        var periodDays = 2;
        
        if (vipType === "vip") {
            skipLimit = 3;
            periodDays = 3;
        } else if (vipType === "start") {
            skipLimit = 5;
            periodDays = 3;
        } else if (vipType === "medium") {
            skipLimit = 4;
            periodDays = 3;
        } else if (vipType === "supreme") {
            skipLimit = 6;
            periodDays = 3;
        } else if (role === "admin" || role === "mod" || role === "owner") {
            // Admin/Mod/Owner sin límite
            skipLimit = 999;
        }
        
        // Verificar contador de skips comprados en el período
        var skipPeriodData = (playerStats[Ss.SK] || "").toString();
        var skipParts = skipPeriodData.split(":");
        var skipDate = skipParts[0];
        var skipCount = parseInt(skipParts[1]) || 0;
        
        var today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
        var periodStart = new Date();
        periodStart.setDate(periodStart.getDate() - (periodDays - 1));
        var periodStartStr = periodStart.toISOString().split('T')[0];
        
        // Si el período expiró, resetear contador
        if (!skipDate || skipDate < periodStartStr) {
            skipDate = today;
            skipCount = 0;
        }
        
        // Verificar si alcanzó el límite
        if (skipCount >= skipLimit) {
            var daysRemaining = periodDays;
            var lastDate = new Date(skipDate);
            var today2 = new Date();
            var daysPassed = Math.floor((today2 - lastDate) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
            daysRemaining = periodDays - daysPassed;
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Alcanzaste el máximo de skips (" + skipLimit + ") en " + periodDays + " días! Intenta en " + Math.max(1, daysRemaining) + " día(s).", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Si el jugador tiene skips asignados (SA) por VIP, consumirlos primero (no cobran)
        var allocatedSkips = Number(playerStats[Ss.SA]) || 0;
        if (allocatedSkips > 0) {
            playerStats[Ss.SA] = allocatedSkips - 1;
            localStorage.setItem(getAuth(player), JSON.stringify(playerStats));
            var authKey = getAuth(player);
            localStorage.setItem('skip_' + authKey, String(Date.now()));
            room.sendAnnouncement("✅ " + player.name + " usó un skip asignado (VIP). Quedan: " + (playerStats[Ss.SA]) + " skips.", null, 0x00FF00, "normal");
            return false;
        }

        // Si no hay skips asignados, proceder a comprar (con límite de compras por período)
        if (playerCoins < skipCost) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ No tienes suficientes coins! Necesitas " + skipCost + " coins, tienes " + playerCoins, player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }

        // Actualizar stats: restar coins y aumentar contador de compras en el período
        playerStats[Ss.CO] = playerCoins - skipCost;
        playerStats[Ss.SK] = skipDate + ":" + (skipCount + 1);
        localStorage.setItem(getAuth(player), JSON.stringify(playerStats));

        // Dar el skip
        var authKey = getAuth(player);
        localStorage.setItem('skip_' + authKey, String(Date.now()));

        room.sendAnnouncement("✅ " + player.name + " compró un skip! 💨 (-25 coins) [" + (skipCount + 1) + "/" + skipLimit + "]", null, 0x00FF00, "normal");
        room.sendAnnouncement("➡️ El skip se usó automáticamente al realizar la compra.", player.id, 0x00FF00, "normal");
        room.sendAnnouncement("💰 Tu balance: " + playerStats[Ss.CO] + " coins", player.id, 0xFFD700, "bold");
        return false;
    } else if (["!comprarvip"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {

            // Formato: !givecoins <cantidad> <@jugador>

            targetName = message[2].replace(/^@/, '');

            targetName = message[1].replace(/^@/, '');
            amount = parseInt(message[2]);
        } else {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Uso correcto: !givecoins <@jugador> <cantidad>", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }

            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ La cantidad debe ser un número positivo mayor a 0!", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        
        var target = findPlayerByName(targetName);

            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Jugador no encontrado: " + targetName, player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }

            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Solo el Owner puede usar este comando!", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        if (!message[1] || !message[2]) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("Uso: !clearcoins <@jugador> <cantidad> o !clearcoins <cantidad> <@jugador>", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        var amount = 0;
        var targetName = "";

        var secondIsNumber = !isNaN(parseInt(message[2]));

    } else if (["!yape"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        // Comando !yape: Transferir monedas entre jugadores normales
        // Uso: !yape <cantidad> @usuario <descripción>
        
        if (message.length < 3) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("Uso: !yape <cantidad> @usuario <descripción>", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        
        var amount = parseInt(message[1]);
        if (isNaN(amount) || amount <= 0) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ La cantidad debe ser un número positivo!", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        if (amount > 150) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Solo puedes enviar hasta 150 coins por !yape", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        // comprobar cooldown de 3 minutos
        var authKey = getAuth(player) || player.id;
        var cdKey = 'yape_cd_' + authKey;
        var last = Number(localStorage.getItem(cdKey)) || 0;
        var now = Date.now();
        if (now - last < 3 * 60 * 1000) {
            var wait = Math.ceil((3 * 60 * 1000 - (now - last)) / 1000);
            room.sendAnnouncement('⏳ Espera ' + wait + 's antes de volver a usar !yape', player.id, 0xFF0000);
            return false;
        }
        localStorage.setItem(cdKey, String(now));
        
        var targetName = message[2].replace(/^@/, '');
        var target = findPlayerByName(targetName);
        if (!target) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Jugador no encontrado: " + targetName, player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        
        var description = message.slice(3).join(' ') || "Sin descripción";
        if (target.id === player.id) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ No puedes yapearte a ti mismo", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        // Obtener stats del remitente (quien usa !yape)
        var senderStats = getPlayerStats(player);
        var senderCoins = Number(senderStats[Ss.CO]) || 0;
        
        // Verificar si tiene suficientes monedas
        if (senderCoins < amount) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("!paso algo, creo que no tienes dinero suficiente para continuar la transacción!!", player.id, 0xFF0000, "bold");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Restar del remitente
        senderStats[Ss.CO] = senderCoins - amount;
        localStorage.setItem(getAuth(player), JSON.stringify(senderStats));
        
        // Sumar al receptor
        var receiverStats = getPlayerStats(target);
        receiverStats[Ss.CO] = (Number(receiverStats[Ss.CO]) || 0) + amount;
        localStorage.setItem(getAuth(target), JSON.stringify(receiverStats));
        
        // Mensajes de confirmación (Morado para YAPEO)
        // Mensaje público: para todos
        room.sendAnnouncement("💜 @" + player.name + " Yapeo a @" + target.name + " " + amount + " coins", null, 0xA020F0, "bold");
        
        // Mensaje privado para quien yapeo
        room.sendAnnouncement("💜 Yapeaste " + amount + " coins a @" + target.name, player.id, 0xA020F0, "normal");
        room.sendAnnouncement("📝 Concepto: " + description, player.id, 0xA020F0, "normal");
        
        // Mensaje privado para quien recibió
        room.sendAnnouncement("💜 @" + player.name + " Te Yapeo " + amount + " coins", target.id, 0xA020F0, "normal");
        room.sendAnnouncement("📝 Concepto: " + description, target.id, 0xA020F0, "normal");
        return false;
    } else if (["!votar"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        // Comando !votar: Apostar coins en un equipo
        // Uso: !votar red <cantidad> o !votar blue <cantidad>
        
        if (!bettingWindowActive) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ La ventana de apuestas está cerrada. Esperemos a la siguiente partida!", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        
        if (message.length < 3) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("Uso: !votar red <cantidad> o !votar blue <cantidad>", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        
        var team = message[1].toLowerCase();
        if (team !== "red" && team !== "blue") {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Team inválido. Usa red o blue", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Verificar si ya ha apostado el máximo de veces hoy
        var betsPeriod = getPlayerBetsCount(player);
        if (betsPeriod >= 10) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Ya has apostado 10 veces en las últimas 3 horas. Límite alcanzado!", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        
        var amount = parseInt(message[2]);
        if (isNaN(amount) || amount <= 0) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ La cantidad debe ser un número positivo!", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        // Obtener stats del jugador
        var stats = getPlayerStats(player);
        var playerCoins = Number(stats[Ss.CO]) || 0;
        var role = stats[Ss.RL];
        var vipType = stats[Ss.VT];
        // comprobar límite según rango/vip
        var limit = getBetLimit(role, vipType);
        if (amount > limit) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Límite de apuesta para tu rango/VIP: " + limit + " coins", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        // Nota: playerCoins se actualizará tras cobrar interés
        
        // calcular interés en función de lo apostado
        var interest = calculateInterest(amount);
        if (playerCoins < interest) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Debes tener al menos " + interest + " coins en tu banca para pagar el interés!", player.id, 0xFF0000, "normal");
            return false;
        }
        // deducir el interés inmediatamente
        stats[Ss.CO] = playerCoins - interest;
        playerCoins -= interest;
        localStorage.setItem(getAuth(player), JSON.stringify(stats));
        room.sendAnnouncement("💸 Interés cobrado: " + interest + " coins. Balance restante: " + stats[Ss.CO] + " coins", player.id, 0xFFD700, "normal");
        
        // Registrar la apuesta
        playerBets[player.id] = {
            team: team,
            amount: amount
        };
        
        // Incrementar contador de apuestas
        incrementPlayerBetsCount(player);
        
        // Confirmar al jugador
        room.sendAnnouncement("💜 Listo votaste por el " + team.toUpperCase() + "!! Esperemos que gane !!", player.id, 0xD3D3D3, "normal");
        
        return false;
    } else if (["!givecolla"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {

            return false;

        } else if (pregunta.includes('que hora es') || pregunta.includes('qué hora es')) {
            var ahora = new Date();
            var hora = ahora.getHours() + ":" + (ahora.getMinutes() < 10 ? '0' : '') + ahora.getMinutes();
            var respuestasHora = [
