        room.sendAnnouncement("ChapuMago🧙‍♂️: " + respuesta, null, 0x87CEEB, "normal");
        
        // OWNER NO registra cooldown del !8ball
        if (!isOwner8Ball) {
            cooldowns[player.id] = Date.now();
        }
        return false;
    }

    // Comando !loginadm ha sido inhabilitado: Owners definidos en ownersAuths
    
    // Comando !report para reportar a un usuario (usa mismo webhook que tickets)
    else if (["!report"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        if (message.length < 3) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Uso: !report @usuario razon", player.id, 0xFF0000, "bold");
            return false;
        }
        var reportadoNombre = message[1];
        var razon = message.slice(2).join(' ');
        if (!reportadoNombre.startsWith('@')) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Usa: !report @usuario razon", player.id, 0xFF0000);
            return false;
        }
        var reportadoName = reportadoNombre.substring(1);
        var reportado = room.getPlayerList().find(p => p.name == reportadoName);
        if (reportado == null) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ El jugador no está en la sala!", player.id, 0xFF0000);
            return false;
        }
        // cooldown 30s (OWNER NO tiene cooldown)
        var reportStats = getPlayerStats(player);
        var isOwnerReport = reportStats && reportStats[Ss.RL] == "owner";
        
        if (!isOwnerReport) {
            var reportCooldownKey = "report_cooldown_" + player.id;
            var lastReportTime = localStorage.getItem(reportCooldownKey);
            var now = Date.now();
            var cooldownTime = 30 * 1000;
            if (lastReportTime && now - Number.parseInt(lastReportTime) < cooldownTime) {
                var remaining = Math.ceil((cooldownTime - (now - Number.parseInt(lastReportTime))) / 1000);
                room.sendAnnouncement("⏳ Espera " + remaining + "s para otro report", player.id, 0xFFAA00);
                return false;
            }
            localStorage.setItem(reportCooldownKey, String(now));
        }
        var timestamp = new Date().toLocaleString();
        var reportadorAuth = getAuth(player);
        var reportadoAuth = getAuth(reportado);
        sendReportToDiscord(player.name + " [" + reportadorAuth + "]", reportado.name + " [" + reportadoAuth + "]", razon, timestamp);
        room.sendAnnouncement("📌 " + player.name + " reportó a " + reportado.name + " - Razón: " + razon, player.id, 0xFFA500, "bold");
        room.sendAnnouncement("✅ Report enviado a Discord", player.id, 0x00FF00, "bold");
        return false;
    }

    // Comando !ticket para abrir un ticket de ayuda (sin @usuario), manda distinto formato al mismo webhook
    else if (["!ticket"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        if (message.length < 2) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Uso: !ticket razon_del_ticket", player.id, 0xFF0000, "bold");
            return false;
        }
        var razonTicket = message.slice(1).join(' ');
        // cooldown 30s (OWNER NO tiene cooldown)
        var tiketStats = getPlayerStats(player);
        var isOwnerTicket = tiketStats && tiketStats[Ss.RL] == "owner";
        
        if (!isOwnerTicket) {
            var tiketCooldownKey = "tiket_cooldown_" + player.id;
            var lastTiketTime = localStorage.getItem(tiketCooldownKey);
            var now2 = Date.now();
            var cooldownTime2 = 30 * 1000;
            if (lastTiketTime && now2 - Number.parseInt(lastTiketTime) < cooldownTime2) {
                var remainingSec = Math.ceil((cooldownTime2 - (now2 - Number.parseInt(lastTiketTime))) / 1000);
                room.sendAnnouncement("⏳ Espera " + remainingSec + " segundo(s) para otro ticket!", player.id, 0xFFAA00);
                return false;
            }
            localStorage.setItem(tiketCooldownKey, String(now2));
        }
        var timestamp2 = new Date().toLocaleString();
        var reportadorAuth2 = getAuth(player);
        sendTicketToDiscord(player.name + " [" + reportadorAuth2 + "]", razonTicket, timestamp2);
        room.sendAnnouncement("🎫 " + player.name + " abrió un TIKET de ayuda - Razón: " + razonTicket, null, 0xFFA500, "bold");
        room.sendAnnouncement("✅ Enviado a Discord", player.id, 0x00FF00, "bold");
        return false;
    }
    
    // Comando !ban para banear por auth o por nombre (@usuario)
    else if (["!ban"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        var stats = getPlayerStats(player);
        var role = stats ? stats[Ss.RL] : "player";
        var modFlag = getAuth(player) && localStorage.getItem('mod_' + getAuth(player));
        var isExecutor = role == "owner" || role == "admin" || role == "mod" || modFlag;
        
        if (!isExecutor) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Solo OWNER, ADMIN y MOD pueden banear", player.id, 0xFF0000);
            return false;
        }

        if (!message[1] || !message[2]) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Uso: !ban @usuario tiempo", player.id, 0xFF0000);
            return false;
        }

        var targetAuth = null;
        var targetPlayer = null;
        if (message[1].startsWith('@')) {
            var nameBan = message[1].substring(1);
            targetPlayer = room.getPlayerList().find(p => p.name == nameBan);
            if (!targetPlayer) { 
                room.sendAnnouncement('❌ Jugador no encontrado: ' + nameBan, player.id, 0xFF0000); 
                return false; 
            }
            targetAuth = getAuth(targetPlayer);
        } else if (message[1].startsWith('#')) {
            targetAuth = message[1].substring(1);
        } else {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Uso: !ban @usuario tiempo", player.id, 0xFF0000);
            return false;
        }

        // Permission check: MOD can only ban users (no admins); ADMIN can ban users/VIPs; OWNER can ban anyone
        if (role != "owner") {
            var tStats = targetAuth ? (localStorage.getItem(targetAuth) ? JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(targetAuth)) : null) : null;
            var tRole = tStats ? tStats[Ss.RL] : "player";
            if (tRole == "owner" || tRole == "admin") {
                room.sendAnnouncement("❌ No puedes banear a " + (tRole == "owner" ? "OWNER" : "ADMIN"), player.id, 0xFF0000);
                return false;
            }
        }

        var banTime = message[2].toLowerCase();
        var banMinutes = 60;
        if (banTime.includes('min')) {
            banMinutes = parseInt(banTime) || 60;
            if (role == 'mod' && banMinutes > 60) { room.sendAnnouncement('❌ MOD máximo 60 minutos', player.id, 0xFF0000); return false; }
        } else if (banTime.includes('hora')) {
            if (role == 'mod') { room.sendAnnouncement('❌ MOD no puede banear por horas', player.id, 0xFF0000); return false; }
            banMinutes = (parseInt(banTime) || 1) * 60;
            if (role == 'admin' && banMinutes > 24 * 60) { room.sendAnnouncement('❌ ADMIN máximo 24 horas', player.id, 0xFF0000); return false; }
        } else if (banTime.includes('dia')) {
            if (role != 'owner') { room.sendAnnouncement('❌ Solo OWNER puede banear por días', player.id, 0xFF0000); return false; }
            banMinutes = (parseInt(banTime) || 1) * 24 * 60;
        } else if (banTime === 'permanente' || banTime === 'perm') {
            if (role != 'owner') { room.sendAnnouncement('❌ Solo OWNER puede banear permanentemente', player.id, 0xFF0000); return false; }
            banMinutes = -1;
        }

        var banKey = 'ban_' + targetAuth;
        var expiry = (banMinutes === -1) ? -1 : Date.now() + banMinutes * 60 * 1000;
        localStorage.setItem(banKey, String(expiry));

        // Kick the player now if online
        if (targetPlayer) {
            try { room.kickPlayer(targetPlayer.id, "🚫 Baneado", true); } catch (e) {}
        }

        room.sendAnnouncement('🚫 ' + (targetPlayer ? targetPlayer.name : ('AUTH ' + targetAuth)) + ' fue BANEADO por ' + (banMinutes === -1 ? 'permanente' : (banMinutes + ' minuto(s)')), null, 0xFF0000, 'bold');
        return false;
    }

    // Alias owner-only: otorgar VIP Supreme directamente
    else if (["!vipsupreme"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        var statsOwner = getPlayerStats(player);
        var isOwnerCmd = statsOwner && statsOwner[Ss.RL] == "owner";
        if (isOwnerCmd) {
            if (message[1] && message[1][0] == "#") {
                var targetId = Number.parseInt(message[1].substring(1));
                var targetPlayer = room.getPlayer(targetId);
                if (targetPlayer != null) {
                    setVipData(getAuth(targetPlayer), 'supreme');
                    room.sendAnnouncement(player.name + " otorgó 💎 VIP SUPREME a " + targetPlayer.name + "!", null, getVipColor('supreme'), "bold");
                } else {
                    room.sendAnnouncement("Jugador no encontrado!", player.id, 0xFF0000);
                }
            } else {
                room.sendAnnouncement("Uso: !vipsupreme #id", player.id, 0xFF0000);
            }
        } else {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Solo el OWNER SUPREMO puede otorgar VIP SUPREME!", player.id, 0xFF0000, "bold");
        }
    }

    // Alias owner-only: otorgar VIP Medium directamente
    else if (["!vipmedium"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        var statsOwner2 = getPlayerStats(player);
        var isOwnerCmd2 = statsOwner2 && statsOwner2[Ss.RL] == "owner";
        if (isOwnerCmd2) {
            if (message[1] && message[1][0] == "#") {
                var targetId2 = Number.parseInt(message[1].substring(1));
                var targetPlayer2 = room.getPlayer(targetId2);
                if (targetPlayer2 != null) {
                    setVipData(getAuth(targetPlayer2), 'medium');
                    room.sendAnnouncement(player.name + " otorgó 🎖️ VIP MEDIUM a " + targetPlayer2.name + "!", null, getVipColor('medium'), "bold");
                } else {
                    room.sendAnnouncement("Jugador no encontrado!", player.id, 0xFF0000);
                }
            } else {
                room.sendAnnouncement("Uso: !vipmedium #id", player.id, 0xFF0000);
            }
        } else {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Solo el OWNER SUPREMO puede otorgar VIP MEDIUM!", player.id, 0xFF0000, "bold");
        }
    }
    
    else if (["!vip"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        var stats = getPlayerStats(player);
        var isOwner = stats && stats[Ss.RL] == "owner";
        
        if (isOwner) {
            if (message[1] && message[1][0] == "#" && message[2]) {
                var targetId = Number.parseInt(message[1].substring(1));
                var targetPlayer = room.getPlayer(targetId);
                var vipType = message[2].toLowerCase();
                
                if (targetPlayer != null && ["supreme", "medium", "vip", "start"].includes(vipType)) {
                    setVipData(getAuth(targetPlayer), vipType);
                    var vipColor = getVipColor(vipType);
                    var vipName = getVipName(vipType);
                    room.sendAnnouncement(player.name + " le otorgó " + vipName + " a " + targetPlayer.name + "!", null, vipColor, "bold");
                } else {
                    room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Uso: !vip #id [supreme|medium|vip|start]", player.id, 0xFF0000);
                }
            } else {
                room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Uso: !vip #id [supreme|medium|vip]", player.id, 0xFF0000);
            }
        } else {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Solo el OWNER SUPREMO puede otorgar VIP!", player.id, 0xFF0000, "bold");
        }
    }
    
    else if (["!skip"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        var stats = getPlayerStats(player);
        var vipData = resetEspIfNeeded(getAuth(player));
        var isVip = vipData && ["supreme", "medium", "vip", "start"].includes(vipData.type);
        var role = stats ? stats[Ss.RL] : "player";
        var isOwnerAdminMod = role == "owner" || role == "admin" || role == "mod" || (getAuth(player) && localStorage.getItem("mod_" + getAuth(player)));
        
        if (!(isVip || isOwnerAdminMod)) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Solo VIP, ADMIN, MOD u OWNER pueden usar !skip", player.id, 0xFF0000, "bold");
            return false;
        }

        if (player.team != Team.SPECTATORS) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Debes estar en la fila de espera para usar !skip", player.id, 0xFF0000, "bold");
            return false;
        }

        // Determinar limite según rol
        var limit;
        if (isOwnerAdminMod) {
            if (role == "owner") {
                limit = 999999; // Ilimitado para owner
            } else {
                limit = 10; // Admin y Mod: 10 usos al día
            }
        } else {
            // VIP limits
            if (vipData.type == "supreme") {
                limit = 10; // VIP Supreme: 10
            } else if (vipData.type == "start") {
                limit = 5;  // VIP Start (owner): 5
            } else if (vipData.type == "medium") {
                limit = 5;  // VIP Medium: 5
            } else {
                limit = 3;  // VIP tienda: 3
            }
        }
        
        var usedCount = vipData ? vipData.espUsed : 0;
        
        if (usedCount < limit) {
            if (!vipData) {
                vipData = { type: null, lastReset: new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10), espUsed: 0 };
            }
            vipData.espUsed++;
            localStorage.setItem("vip_" + getAuth(player), JSON.stringify(vipData));
            
            // Guardar marca de skip para que se reordene en la fila de SPECTATORS
            localStorage.setItem("skip_" + getAuth(player), String(Date.now()));
            // Reordenar la fila de espera (updateTeams() ordena por skip_)
            updateTeams();
            
            var remaining = limit - vipData.espUsed;
            var vipName;
            var message_color;
            
            if (isOwnerAdminMod) {
                if (role == "owner") {
                    vipName = "👑 OWNER";
                    message_color = 0xFFD700;
                } else if (role == "admin") {
                    vipName = "👨‍💼 ADMIN";
                    message_color = 0xFF7900;
                } else {
                    vipName = "🛡️ MOD";
                    message_color = 0x6ECAFF;
                }
            } else {
                vipName = getVipName(vipData.type);
                message_color = getVipColor(vipData.type);
            }
            
            if (role == "owner") {
                room.sendAnnouncement("⭐ " + player.name + " (" + vipName + ") saltó la fila!", null, message_color, "bold");
            } else {
                room.sendAnnouncement("⭐ " + player.name + " (" + vipName + ") saltó la fila! | Usos restantes: " + remaining + "/" + limit, null, message_color, "bold");
            }
        } else {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ " + player.name + ", ya agotaste tus " + limit + " usos de !skip por hoy. Vuelve mañana!", player.id, 0xFF0000, "bold");
        }
        return false;
    }
    
    else if (["!listadeadministradores", "!listadm"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        try {
            var ownersNamesList = [];
            var adminsNamesList = [];
            var modsNamesList = [];

            // Obtener Owners desde ownersAuths
            if (ownersAuths && ownersAuths.length > 0) {
                ownersAuths.forEach(function(a) {
                    try { 
                        var name = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(a))[Ss.NK]; 
                        if (name && name !== "undefined" && name.trim() !== "") {
                            ownersNamesList.push(name);
                        }
                    } catch (e) {}
                });
            }

            var admins = new Set();
            var mods = new Set();

            // Online players with admin flag
            room.getPlayerList().forEach(function(p) {
                if (p.id == 0) return;
                if (p.admin) admins.add(p.name);
                var a = getAuth(p);
                if (a && localStorage.getItem('mod_' + a)) mods.add(p.name);
            });

            // Persistent admins/mods in storage (sin mostrar auths)
            Object.keys(localStorage).forEach(function(key) {
                if (["player_name","view_mode","geo","avatar","player_auth_key"].includes(key)) return;
                try {
                    var s = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key));
                    if (!s) return;
                    var playerName = s[Ss.NK];
                    if (!playerName || playerName === "undefined" || playerName.trim() === "") return;
                    if (s[Ss.RL] == 'admin') admins.add(playerName);
                    if (s[Ss.RL] == 'mod' || localStorage.getItem('mod_' + key)) mods.add(playerName);
                } catch (e) {}
            });

            // Convertir Sets a Arrays
            adminsNamesList = Array.from(admins);
            modsNamesList = Array.from(mods);

            // Enviar anuncios solo si hay contenido
            room.sendAnnouncement("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━", player.id, 0xFFD700, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement("📋 LISTA DE ADMINISTRACIÓN", player.id, 0xFFD700, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━", player.id, 0xFFD700, "bold");

            // OWNERS
            if (ownersNamesList.length > 0) {
                room.sendAnnouncement("", player.id, 0xFFD700, "normal");
                room.sendAnnouncement("👑 LISTA DE OWNERS:", player.id, 0xFFD700, "bold");
                for (var i = 0; i < ownersNamesList.length; i++) {
                    room.sendAnnouncement((i + 1) + ". " + ownersNamesList[i], player.id, 0xFFD700, "normal");
                }
            }

            // ADMINS
            if (adminsNamesList.length > 0) {
                room.sendAnnouncement("", player.id, 0xFF7900, "normal");
                room.sendAnnouncement("🔐 LISTA DE ADMINS:", player.id, 0xFF7900, "bold");
                for (var i = 0; i < adminsNamesList.length; i++) {
                    room.sendAnnouncement((i + 1) + ". " + adminsNamesList[i], player.id, 0xFF7900, "normal");
                }
            }

            // MODS
            if (modsNamesList.length > 0) {
                room.sendAnnouncement("", player.id, 0x6ECAFF, "normal");
                room.sendAnnouncement("🛡️ LISTA DE MODS:", player.id, 0x6ECAFF, "bold");
                for (var i = 0; i < modsNamesList.length; i++) {
                    room.sendAnnouncement((i + 1) + ". " + modsNamesList[i], player.id, 0x6ECAFF, "normal");
                }
            }

            room.sendAnnouncement("", player.id, 0xFFD700, "normal");
            room.sendAnnouncement("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━", player.id, 0xFFD700, "bold");
        } catch (e) {
            room.sendAnnouncement('❌ Error al generar la lista de administradores', player.id, 0xFF0000);
        }
        return false;
    }
    
        else if (["!tops"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
            var tableau = [];
            try {
                Object.keys(localStorage).forEach(function(key) {
                    if (!["player_name", "view_mode", "geo", "avatar", "player_auth_key"].includes(key)) {
                        try {
                            var playerData = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key));
                            if (playerData && playerData[Ss.NK] && !isNaN(playerData[Ss.WI]) && playerData[Ss.NK] !== "undefined" && playerData[Ss.NK].trim() !== "") {
                                tableau.push([playerData[Ss.NK], playerData[Ss.WI] || 0, key]);
                            }
                        } catch (e) {}
                    }
                });
            } catch {}
        
            tableau.sort(function(a, b) {
                return b[1] - a[1];
            });
            
            if (players.length < 2 * maxTeamSize) {
                room.sendAnnouncement("⚠️ FALTAN " + (2 * maxTeamSize - players.length) + " JUGADORES para poder rankear!", player.id, 0xFF0000);
            }
        
            // Verificar si es nuevo top y mostrar felicitación personalizada
            var playerRankKey = "playerRank_" + getAuth(player);
            var previousRank = localStorage.getItem(playerRankKey);
            var currentRank = -1;
            
            // Encontrar posición actual del jugador
            for (var j = 0; j < tableau.length; j++) {
                if (tableau[j][0] == player.name) {
                    currentRank = j;
                    break;
                }
            }
            
            // Si llegó a top 3 y no estaba antes
            if (currentRank >= 0 && currentRank < 3) {
                if (!previousRank || parseInt(previousRank) >= 3) {
                    // Es nuevo en top 3
                    if (currentRank == 0) {
                        room.sendAnnouncement("👑 ¡¡ERES EL MEJOR!! @" + player.name + " LLEGASTE AL TOP 1 - ERES UNA LEYENDA EN EL LHOST 🏆", null, 0xFFD700, "bold");
                    } else if (currentRank == 1) {
                        room.sendAnnouncement("🥈 CASI CASI... @" + player.name + " ERES EL TOP 2 - CASI LE GANAS AL REY PERO TODAVIA TE FALTA MAS HAMBRE", null, 0xC0C0C0, "bold");
                    } else if (currentRank == 2) {
                        room.sendAnnouncement("🥉 ESFUERZATE MAS @" + player.name + " - ERES TOP 3 PERO FALTA MUCHO PARA LLEGAR AL TRONO, DALE ESA GARRA", null, 0xCD7F32, "bold");
                    }
                }
                localStorage.setItem(playerRankKey, String(currentRank));
            } else if (currentRank >= 3 && previousRank && parseInt(previousRank) < 3) {
                // Salió del top 3
                localStorage.removeItem(playerRankKey);
            }
            
            room.sendAnnouncement("━━━━━━━━━ TOPS 🏆 VICIADOS ━━━━━━━━━", player.id, 0xFFD700, "bold");
            for (var i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
                if (tableau[i] && tableau[i][0]) {
                    var medalEmoji = i === 0 ? "🥇" : i === 1 ? "🥈" : i === 2 ? "🥉" : (i + 1);
                    var playerAuth = tableau[i][2];
                    try {
                        var playerStats = localStorage.getItem(playerAuth) ? JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(playerAuth)) : null;
                        var playerRole = playerStats && playerStats[Ss.RL] ? playerStats[Ss.RL] : "player";
                        var roleEmoji = "";
                        
                        if (playerRole == "owner") {
                            roleEmoji = "👑";
                        } else if (playerRole == "admin") {
                            roleEmoji = "🔐";
                        } else if (playerRole == "mod") {
                            roleEmoji = "🛡️";
                        }
                        
                        var displayName = tableau[i][0] + (roleEmoji ? " " + roleEmoji : "");
                        room.sendAnnouncement(medalEmoji + " #" + (i + 1) + " - " + displayName + ": " + tableau[i][1] + " wins", player.id, 0xFFAE00, "normal");
                    } catch (e) {
                        room.sendAnnouncement(medalEmoji + " #" + (i + 1) + " - " + tableau[i][0] + ": " + tableau[i][1] + " wins", player.id, 0xFFAE00, "normal");
                    }
                } else {
                    room.sendAnnouncement("#" + (i + 1) + " - ?", player.id, 0xABAEA7, "normal");
                }
            }
            room.sendAnnouncement("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━", player.id, 0xFFD700, "bold");
            return false;
    }

    else if (["!mutes", "!mutelist"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        var cstm = "[BO] Lista de mutados : ";
        for (var i = 0; i < extendedP.length; i++) {
            if (room.getPlayer(extendedP[i][eP.ID]) != null && getMute(room.getPlayer(extendedP[i][eP.ID]))) {
                if (140 - cstm.length < (room.getPlayer(extendedP[i][eP.ID]).name + "[" + (extendedP[i][eP.ID]) + "], ").length) {
                    room.sendChat(cstm, player.id);
                    cstm = "... ";
                }
                cstm += room.getPlayer(extendedP[i][eP.ID]).name + "[" + (extendedP[i][eP.ID]) + "], ";
            }
        }
        if (cstm == "[BO] Lista de mutados : ") {
            room.sendChat("[BO] No hay nadie en la lista de mutados !", player.id);
            return false;
        }
        cstm = cstm.substring(0, cstm.length - 2);
        cstm += ".";
        room.sendChat(cstm, player.id);
    } else if (["!mute"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        var stats = getPlayerStats(player);
        var role = stats ? stats[Ss.RL] : "player";
        var modFlag = getAuth(player) && localStorage.getItem('mod_' + getAuth(player));
        var isExecutor = role == "owner" || role == "admin" || role == "mod" || modFlag;
        
        if (!isExecutor) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Solo OWNER, ADMIN y MOD pueden mutear", player.id, 0xFF0000);
            return false;
        }

        if (!message[1]) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Uso: !mute @usuario [tiempo]", player.id, 0xFF0000);
            return false;
        }

        var targetPlayer = null;
        var targetAuth = null;
        if (message[1].startsWith('@')) {
            var targetName = message[1].substring(1);
            targetPlayer = room.getPlayerList().find(p => p.name == targetName);
            if (!targetPlayer) {
                room.sendAnnouncement('❌ Jugador no encontrado: ' + targetName, player.id, 0xFF0000);
                return false;
            }
            targetAuth = getAuth(targetPlayer);
        } else {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Uso: !mute @usuario [tiempo]", player.id, 0xFF0000);
            return false;
        }

        // Permission check: MOD can only mute users (no admins); ADMIN can mute users/VIPs; OWNER can mute anyone
        if (role != "owner") {
            var tStats = targetAuth ? (localStorage.getItem(targetAuth) ? JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(targetAuth)) : null) : null;
            var tRole = tStats ? tStats[Ss.RL] : "player";
            if (tRole == "owner" || tRole == "admin") {
                room.sendAnnouncement("❌ No puedes mutear a " + (tRole == "owner" ? "OWNER" : "ADMIN"), player.id, 0xFF0000);
                return false;
            }
        }

        var timeToken = message[2] ? message[2].toLowerCase() : "3min";
        var muteMinutes = 3;
        if (timeToken.includes('min')) {
            muteMinutes = parseInt(timeToken) || 3;
            if (role == 'mod' && muteMinutes > 60) { room.sendAnnouncement('❌ MOD máximo 60 minutos', player.id, 0xFF0000); return false; }
        } else if (timeToken.includes('hora')) {
            if (role == 'mod') { room.sendAnnouncement('❌ MOD no puede mutear por horas', player.id, 0xFF0000); return false; }
            muteMinutes = (parseInt(timeToken) || 1) * 60;

            if (role == 'admin' && muteMinutes > 24 * 60) { room.sendAnnouncement('❌ ADMIN máximo 24 horas', player.id, 0xFF0000); return false; }
        } else if (timeToken.includes('dia')) {
            if (role != 'owner') { room.sendAnnouncement('❌ Solo OWNER puede mutear por días', player.id, 0xFF0000); return false; }
            muteMinutes = (parseInt(timeToken) || 1) * 24 * 60;
        } else if (timeToken === 'permanente' || timeToken === 'perm') {
            if (role != 'owner') { room.sendAnnouncement('❌ Solo OWNER puede mutear permanentemente', player.id, 0xFF0000); return false; }
            muteMinutes = -1;
        }

        var muteKey = 'mute_' + targetAuth;
        var expiry = (muteMinutes === -1) ? -1 : Date.now() + muteMinutes * 60 * 1000;
        localStorage.setItem(muteKey, String(expiry));

        if (targetPlayer) {
            try { setMute(targetPlayer, true); } catch (e) {}
        }

        room.sendAnnouncement('🔇 ' + (targetPlayer ? targetPlayer.name : ('AUTH ' + targetAuth)) + ' fue MUTEADO por ' + (muteMinutes === -1 ? 'permanente' : (muteMinutes + ' minuto(s)')), null, 0xFF7900, 'bold');
        return false;

    } else if (["!unmute"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        if (!(player.admin || player.id == 0 || (getPlayerStats(player) && getPlayerStats(player)[Ss.RL] == 'owner'))) return false;
        if (!message[1]) return false;
        // Allow @username or #auth
        var targetAuth2 = null;
        var targetPlayer2 = null;
        if (message[1].startsWith('@')) {
            var name2 = message[1].substring(1);
            targetPlayer2 = room.getPlayerList().find(p => p.name == name2);
            if (!targetPlayer2) { room.sendAnnouncement('❌ Jugador no encontrado: ' + name2, player.id, 0xFF0000); return false; }
            targetAuth2 = getAuth(targetPlayer2);
        } else if (message[1].startsWith('#')) {
            targetAuth2 = message[1].substring(1);
        } else if (message[1] == 'all') {
            // clear in-memory mutes and persistent mutes
            extendedP.forEach(ePlayer => ePlayer[eP.MUTE] = false);
            Object.keys(localStorage).forEach(function(k){ if (k.startsWith('mute_')) localStorage.removeItem(k); });
            room.sendChat('Todos fueron desmuteados');
            return false;
        }

        if (targetAuth2 && localStorage.getItem('mute_' + targetAuth2)) localStorage.removeItem('mute_' + targetAuth2);
        if (targetPlayer2) setMute(targetPlayer2, false);
        room.sendAnnouncement('🔊 ' + (targetPlayer2 ? targetPlayer2.name : ('AUTH ' + targetAuth2)) + ' fue DESMUTEADO', null, 0x73EC59);
        return false;
    } else if (["!ban"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        // Comando !ban: Solo ADMIN/OWNER pueden banear
        var role = getPlayerStats(player) ? getPlayerStats(player)[Ss.RL] : "player";
        if (role != "admin" && role != "owner") {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Solo ADMIN/OWNER pueden usar !ban", player.id, 0xFF0000, "bold");
            return false;
        }
        
        if (!message[1]) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("Uso: !ban @usuario horas [razón]", player.id, 0xFF7900);
            return false;
        }
        
        var targetName = message[1].startsWith('@') ? message[1].substring(1) : message[1];
        var targetPlayer = room.getPlayerList().find(p => p.name == targetName && p.id != 0);
        if (!targetPlayer) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Jugador no encontrado: " + targetName, player.id, 0xFF0000);
            return false;
        }
        
        var horas = parseInt(message[2]) || 1;
        var duracion = horas == -1 ? "Permanente" : horas + " horas";
        var razon = message.slice(3).join(' ') || "Sin especificar";
        
        // Crear ban en localStorage
        var banDuration = horas == -1 ? -1 : horas * 60 * 60 * 1000; // Convertir a ms
        var banKey = "ban_" + targetPlayer.auth;
        var banInfo = {
            reason: razon,
            duration: banDuration,
            bannedAt: Date.now(),
            bannedBy: player.name
        };
        localStorage.setItem(banKey, JSON.stringify(banInfo));
        
        var timestamp = new Date().toLocaleString();
        room.sendAnnouncement("🚫 " + targetPlayer.name + " fue BANEADO por " + duracion + " | Razón: " + razon, null, 0xFF0000, "bold");
        room.kickPlayer(targetPlayer.id, "🚫 Has sido BANEADO | Razón: " + razon + " | Duración: " + duracion, true);
        
        // Enviar reporte a Discord
        sendBanReportToDiscord(player.name, targetPlayer.name, duracion, razon, timestamp);
        return false;
        
    } else if (["!kick"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
        // Comando !kick: Solo ADMIN/OWNER pueden patear
        var role = getPlayerStats(player) ? getPlayerStats(player)[Ss.RL] : "player";
        if (role != "admin" && role != "owner") {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Solo ADMIN/OWNER pueden usar !kick", player.id, 0xFF0000, "bold");
            return false;
        }
        
        if (!message[1]) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("Uso: !kick @usuario [razón]", player.id, 0xFF7900);
            return false;
        }
        
        var targetName = message[1].startsWith('@') ? message[1].substring(1) : message[1];
        var targetPlayer = room.getPlayerList().find(p => p.name == targetName && p.id != 0);
        if (!targetPlayer) {
            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Jugador no encontrado: " + targetName, player.id, 0xFF0000);
            return false;
        }
        
        var razon = message.slice(2).join(' ') || "Sin especificar";
        var timestamp = new Date().toLocaleString();
        
        room.sendAnnouncement("👢 " + targetPlayer.name + " fue PATEADO | Razón: " + razon, null, 0xFF9900, "bold");
        room.kickPlayer(targetPlayer.id, "👢 Has sido PATEADO | Razón: " + razon, false);
        
        // Enviar reporte a Discord
        sendKickReportToDiscord(player.name, targetPlayer.name, razon, timestamp);
        return false;

            room.sendAnnouncement("❌ Solo OWNER puede usar !giveadmin", player.id, 0xFF0000, "bold");

        }
        var cstm = "[BO] Lista de baneados : ";
        for (var i = 0; i < banList.length; i++) {
            if (140 - cstm.length < (banList[i][0] + "[" + (banList[i][1]) + "], ").length) {
                room.sendChat(cstm, player.id);
                cstm = "... ";

            cstm += banList[i][0] + "[" + (banList[i][1]) + "], ";

                banList = [];
            }
            if (message.length == 2) {
                if (!Number.isNaN(Number.parseInt(message[1]))) {
                    if (Number.parseInt(message[1]) > 0) {
                        ID = Number.parseInt(message[1]);
                        room.clearBan(ID);
                        if (banList.length != banList.filter((array) => array[1] != ID)) {

                        }, 20);

                }

    if (xingo.includes(message[0])) {
        room.kickPlayer(player.id, "❌ El racismo no esta tolerado en esta sala.", false);
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("Player " + player.name + " falou merda"), player.id, Cor.Warn, "italic");
        return false;
    }
    if (xingo.includes(message[1])) {
        room.kickPlayer(player.id, "❌ El racismo no se tolera en esta sala.", false);
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("Player " + player.name + " falou merda"), player.id, Cor.Warn, "italic");
        return false;
    }
    if (xingo.includes(message[2])) {
        room.kickPlayer(player.id, "❌ El racismo no se tolera en esta sala.", false);
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("Player " + player.name + " falou merda"), player.id, Cor.Warn, "italic");
        return false;
    }
    if (xingo.includes(message[3])) {
        room.kickPlayer(player.id, "❌ El racismo no se tolera en esta sala.", false);
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("Player " + player.name + " falou merda"), player.id, Cor.Warn, "italic");
        return false;
    }
    if (xingo.includes(message[4])) {
        room.kickPlayer(player.id, "❌ El racismo no se tolera en esta sala..", false);
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("Player " + player.name + " falou merda"), player.id, Cor.Warn, "italic");
        return false;
    }
    if (xingo.includes(message[5])) {
        room.kickPlayer(player.id, "❌ El racismo no se tolera en esta sala..", false);
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("Player " + player.name + " falou merda"), player.id, Cor.Warn, "italic");
        return false;
    }
    if (regex.includes(message[0])) {
        room.sendAnnouncement("sin malas palabras, " + player.name, player.id, Cor.Warn, "italic", 2);
        return false;
    }
    if (regex.includes(message[1])) {
        room.sendAnnouncement("Sin malas palabras, " + player.name, player.id, Cor.Warn, "italic", 2);
        return false;
    }
    if (regex.includes(message[2])) {
        room.sendAnnouncement("Sin palabrotas, " + player.name, player.id, Cor.Warn, "italic", 2);
        return false;
    }
    if (regex.includes(message[3])) {
        room.sendAnnouncement("Sin palabrotas, " + player.name, player.id, Cor.Warn, "italic", 2);
        return false;
    }
    if (regex.includes(message[4])) {
        room.sendAnnouncement("Sin palabrotas, " + player.name, player.id, Cor.Warn, "italic", 2);
        return false;
    }
    if (regex.includes(message[5])) {
        room.sendAnnouncement("Sin palabrotas, " + player.name, player.id, Cor.Warn, "italic", 2);
        return false;
    }

    // SISTEMA ANTI-LINKS: Solo OWNER y ADMIN pueden mandar links
    try {
        var playerStats = localStorage.getItem(getAuth(player)) ? JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(getAuth(player))) : null;
        var playerRole = playerStats ? playerStats[Ss.RL] : "player";
        var isModFlag = localStorage.getItem("mod_" + getAuth(player)) ? true : false;
        var hasPermission = playerRole == "owner" || playerRole == "admin";
        
        // Verificar si el mensaje contiene un link
        var hasLink = false;
        var messageLower = originalMessage.toLowerCase();
        
        // Detectar links generales
        for (var i = 0; i < message.length; i++) {
            if (link.includes(message[i])) {
                hasLink = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        
        // Detectar específicamente palabras clave de Discord
        if (!hasLink && (messageLower.includes("discord") || messageLower.includes("discord.gg"))) {
            hasLink = true;
        }
        
        // Si contiene link y NO es OWNER o ADMIN, bloquear el mensaje silenciosamente
        if (hasLink && !hasPermission) {
            return false; // El mensaje simplemente desaparece sin mostrar nada
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log("Error en sistema anti-links: " + e);
    }

    if (message[0][0] == "!") { // faz nao aparecer o comando usado no chat
        return false;
    }

    if (TeamR.length != 0 && TeamB.length != 0 && inChooseMode) { // modo de escolher o time
        if (player.id == TeamR[0].id || player.id == TeamB[0].id) { // aqui nós nos importamos se é um dos capitães escolhendo
            if (TeamR.length <= TeamB.length && player.id == TeamR[0].id) { // aqui nós nos importamos se é red turn && red cap falando
                if (["top", "auto"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
                    room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[0].id, Team.RED);
                    redCaptainChoice = "top";
                    clearTimeout(timeOutCap);
                    room.sendAnnouncement(player.name + " optó por elegir el primero de la lista!", null, 0x55bae2, "normal");
                    return false;
                } else if (["random", "rand"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
                    var r = getRandomInt(teamS.length);
                    room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[r].id, Team.RED);
                    redCaptainChoice = "random";
                    clearTimeout(timeOutCap);
                    room.sendAnnouncement(player.name + " optó por elegir un equipo al azar", null, 0x55bae2, "normal");
                    return false;
                } else if (["bottom", "bot"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
                    room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[teamS.length - 1].id, Team.RED);
                    redCaptainChoice = "bottom";
                    clearTimeout(timeOutCap);
                    room.sendAnnouncement(player.name + " optó por elegir el último de la lista!", null, 0x55bae2, "normal");
                    return false;
                } else if (!Number.isNaN(Number.parseInt(message[0]))) {
                    if (Number.parseInt(message[0]) > teamS.length || Number.parseInt(message[0]) < 1) {
                        room.sendAnnouncement("[⚠️] ¡Ops! El número que ha elegido no es válido.", player.id, null, 0xfaca29, "normal");
                        return false;
                    } else {
                        room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[Number.parseInt(message[0]) - 1].id, Team.RED);
                        room.sendAnnouncement(player.name + " recluto o(a): " + teamS[Number.parseInt(message[0]) - 1].name + " !", null, 0x55bae2, "normal");
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (TeamR.length > TeamB.length && player.id == TeamB[0].id) { // aqui nós nos importamos se é red turn && red cap falando
                if (["top", "auto"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
                    room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[0].id, Team.BLUE);
                    blueCaptainChoice = "top";
                    clearTimeout(timeOutCap);
                    room.sendAnnouncement(player.name + " optó por elegir el primero de la lista!", null, 0x55bae2, "normal");
                    return false;
                } else if (["random", "rand"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
                    room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[getRandomInt(teamS.length)].id, Team.BLUE);
                    blueCaptainChoice = "random";
                    clearTimeout(timeOutCap);
                    room.sendAnnouncement(player.name + " eligió elegir un equipo al azar", null, 0x55bae2, "normal");
                    return false;
                } else if (["bottom", "bot"].includes(message[0].toLowerCase())) {
                    room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[teamS.length - 1].id, Team.BLUE);
                    blueCaptainChoice = "bottom";
                    clearTimeout(timeOutCap);
                    room.sendAnnouncement(player.name + " opto por elegir al ultimo de la lista!", null, 0x55bae2, "normal");
                    return false;
                } else if (!Number.isNaN(Number.parseInt(message[0]))) {
                    if (Number.parseInt(message[0]) > teamS.length || Number.parseInt(message[0]) < 1) {
                        room.sendAnnouncement("[⚠️] Ops! el numero que pones es invalido.", player.id, null, 0xfaca29, "normal");
                        return false;
                    } else {
                        room.setPlayerTeam(teamS[Number.parseInt(message[0]) - 1].id, Team.BLUE);
                        room.sendAnnouncement(player.name + " recrutou o(a): " + teamS[Number.parseInt(message[0]) - 1].name + "!", null, 0x55bae2, "normal");
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (getMute(player)) {
        room.sendChat("Te encuentras muteado!", player.id);
        return false;
    }
    // unified chat formatting inserted immediately after mute check
    if (localStorage.getItem(getAuth(player))) {
        stats = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(getAuth(player)));
        // unified header for every message: medal, role/vip/rank, wins
        var vipType = stats[Ss.VT];
        var vipExp = stats[Ss.VE];
        var winsCount = Number(stats[Ss.WI]) || 0;
        var modFlag = localStorage.getItem('mod_' + getAuth(player)) ? true : false;
        var chatColor = 0xFFFFFF;
        // determine medal
        var medal = "";
        try {
            var top3 = getTop3Wins();
            for (var i = 0; i < Math.min(3, top3.length); i++) {
                if (top3[i][0] === player.name) {
                    if (i === 0) medal = "🥇";
                    else if (i === 1) medal = "🥈";
                    else if (i === 2) medal = "🥉";
                    break;
                }
            }
        } catch(e) {}

        var prefix = "";
        if (stats[Ss.RL] === "owner") {
            prefix = "👑 - [🏆: " + winsCount + "] Owner";
            chatColor = 0xFFD700;
        } else if (stats[Ss.RL] === "admin") {
            prefix = "🔐 - [🏆: " + winsCount + "] Admin";
            chatColor = 0xFF7900;
        } else if (stats[Ss.RL] === "mod" || modFlag) {
            prefix = "🛡️ - [🏆: " + winsCount + "] Mod";
            chatColor = 0x6ECAFF;
        } else if (vipType && vipExp && Date.now() < vipExp) {
            prefix = getVipName(vipType) + " - [🏆: " + winsCount + "]";
            chatColor = getVipColor(vipType) || getRankColor(stats);
        } else {
            prefix = getRankPrefix(stats); // includes wins and rank name
            chatColor = getRankColor(stats);
        }
        if (medal) prefix = medal + " " + prefix;
        announcement = prefix + " · " + player.name + ": " + originalMessage;
        room.sendAnnouncement(announcement, null, chatColor);
        return false;
    } else {
        room.sendAnnouncement("❌ " + player.name + ": " + originalMessage, null, 0xABAEA7);
        return false;
    }
    if (localStorage.getItem(getAuth(player))) {
        stats = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(getAuth(player)));
        var announcement = "";
        var chatColor = 0xFFFFFF;
        var playerAuth = getAuth(player);
        var modFlag = localStorage.getItem("mod_" + playerAuth) ? true : false;
        
        // VERIFICAR PRIMERO SI TIENE EL RANGO TE VERDE (rango permanente y decorativo)
        if (stats[Ss.DU]) {
            // Rango: [✤𝘛𝘦_𝘝𝘦𝘳𝘥𝘦♡] con colores rosado (Uraraka) y verde (Deku)
            room.sendAnnouncement("[✤𝘛𝘦_𝘝𝘦𝘳𝘥𝘦♡] ✨", null, 0xEA5EC6, "bold");  // Rosado Uraraka
            room.sendAnnouncement(player.name + ": " + originalMessage, null, 0x2B7A5A, "normal");  // Verde Deku
            return false;
        }
        
        // Verificar si es OWNER
        if (stats[Ss.RL] == "owner") {
            announcement = "👑 [OWNER] ";
            chatColor = 0xFFD700;
            announcement += player.name + ": " + originalMessage;
            room.sendAnnouncement(announcement, null, chatColor);
            return false;
        }
        
        // Verificar si es ADMIN
        if (stats[Ss.RL] == "admin" || stats[Ss.RL] == "master") {
            announcement = "🔐 [ADMIN] ";
            chatColor = 0xFF7900;
            announcement += player.name + ": " + originalMessage;
            room.sendAnnouncement(announcement, null, chatColor);
            return false;
        }
        
        // Verificar si es MOD (puede venir de flag separada)
        if (stats[Ss.RL] == "mod" || modFlag) {
            if (stats[Ss.RL] == "owner") {
                announcement = "👑 [OWNER] ";
                if (modFlag) announcement += "MOD ";
            } else if (stats[Ss.RL] == "admin") {
                announcement = "🔐 [ADMIN] ";
                if (modFlag) announcement += "MOD ";
            } else {
                announcement = "MOD ";
            }
            chatColor = 0x6ECAFF;
            announcement += player.name + ": " + originalMessage;
            room.sendAnnouncement(announcement, null, chatColor);
            return false;
        }
        
        // Verificar si está en Top 3 (mostrar medalla + rol/vip/rank)
        var top3 = getTop3Wins();
        var isInTop3 = false;
        var medalEmoji = "";
        var topPosition = -1;
        for (var i = 0; i < top3.length; i++) {
            if (top3[i][0] == player.name) {
                isInTop3 = true;
                topPosition = i;
                if (i == 0) medalEmoji = "🥇";
                else if (i == 1) medalEmoji = "🥈";
                else if (i == 2) medalEmoji = "🥉";
                break;
            }
        }
        
        if (isInTop3) {
            announcement = medalEmoji + " ";
            
            // Determinar el rol/vip/rank y sus propiedades
            if (stats[Ss.RL] == "owner") {
                announcement += "👑 (Owner) ";
                chatColor = 0xFFD700;
            } else if (stats[Ss.RL] == "admin") {
                announcement += "🔐 (Admin) ";
                chatColor = 0xFF7900;
            } else if (stats[Ss.RL] == "mod" || modFlag) {
                announcement += "🛡️ (Mod) ";
                chatColor = 0x6ECAFF;
            } else {
                // Verificar VIP desde stats[Ss.VT]
                var vipType2 = stats[Ss.VT];
                var vipExp2 = stats[Ss.VE];
                
                if (vipType2 && vipExp2 && Date.now() < vipExp2) {
                    // VIP aún es válido
                    var vipName2 = getVipName(vipType2);
                    var vipColor2 = getVipColor(vipType2);
                    announcement += vipName2 + " ";
                    chatColor = vipColor2;
                } else {
                    // Mostrar rank por wins
                    if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 500) {
                        announcement += "🏆 Immortal ";
                        chatColor = 0xE0E0FF;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 450) {
                        announcement += "🌟 Galactic ";
                        chatColor = 0xFFCCFF;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 425) {
                        announcement += "🔱 Supreme ";
                        chatColor = 0xCCFFCC;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 400) {
                        announcement += "🌌 Cosmic ";
                        chatColor = 0xCCFFFF;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 375) {
                        announcement += "⚔️ Titan ";
                        chatColor = 0xFFE0CC;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 350) {
                        announcement += "🐉 Dragon ";
                        chatColor = 0xFFCCCC;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 333) {
                        announcement += "👴 Chapulin ";
                        chatColor = 0xFFFFCC;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 300) {
                        announcement += "👹 Legend ";
                        chatColor = 0xCCFF99;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 288) {
                        announcement += "🏹 Hanzo ";
                        chatColor = 0x99CCFF;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 250) {
                        announcement += "😈 Overlord ";
                        chatColor = 0xFF99CC;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 200) {
                        announcement += "🌟 Prodigy ";
                        chatColor = 0xCC99FF;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 150) {
                        announcement += "🔥 Beast ";
                        chatColor = 0xFFCC99;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 100) {
                        announcement += "👑 Elite ";
                        chatColor = 0xDDEEFF;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 75) {
                        announcement += "⭐ Killer ";
                        chatColor = 0xEEFFDD;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 50) {
                        announcement += "💎 Platinum ";
                        chatColor = 0xF0F0F0;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 30) {
                        announcement += "🌕 Gold ";
                        chatColor = 0xFFF8DC;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 15) {
                        announcement += "🗿 Silver ";
                        chatColor = 0xF8F8FF;
                    } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 5) {
                        announcement += "🍂 Bronze ";
                        chatColor = 0xFAEBD7;
                    } else {
                        announcement += "💫 Principiante ";
                        chatColor = 0xEFEFEF;
                    }
                }
            }
            
            announcement += player.name + ": " + originalMessage;
            room.sendAnnouncement(announcement, null, chatColor);
            return false;
        }
        
        // Verificar si es VIP (no es ni Top 3, ni rol especial)
        var vipType = stats[Ss.VT];
        var vipExp = stats[Ss.VE];
        
        if (vipType && vipExp && Date.now() < vipExp) {
            // VIP aún es válido
            var vipName = getVipName(vipType);
            var vipColor = getVipColor(vipType);
            announcement = vipName + " · ";
            chatColor = vipColor;
            announcement += player.name + ": " + originalMessage;
            room.sendAnnouncement(announcement, null, chatColor);
            return false;
        }
        
        // Si no es nada especial, mostrar por wins
        if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 333) {
            announcement = "[👴] - [🏆: " + stats[Ss.WI] +"]  Chapulin · ";
            chatColor = 0xFFD700;
        } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 300) {
            announcement = "[👹] - [🏆: " + stats[Ss.WI] +"]  Legend · ";
            chatColor = 0xFFD700;
        } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 288) {
            announcement = "[🏹] - [🏆: " + stats[Ss.WI] +"]  Hanzo · ";
            chatColor = 0xFFD700;
        } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 250) {
            announcement = "[😈] - [🏆: " + stats[Ss.WI] +"]  Overlord · ";
            chatColor = 0xFF6B9D;
        } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 200) {
            announcement = "[🌟] - [🏆: " + stats[Ss.WI] +"]  Prodigy · ";
            chatColor = 0x9370DB;
        } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 150) {
            announcement = "[🔥] - [🏆: " + stats[Ss.WI] +"]  Beast · ";
            chatColor = 0xFF4500;
        } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 100) {
            announcement = "[👑] - [🏆: " + stats[Ss.WI] +"]  Elite · ";
            chatColor = 0xE5D4C1;
        } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 75) {
            announcement = "[⭐] - [🏆: " + stats[Ss.WI] +"]  Killer · ";
            chatColor = 0xE5D4C1;
        } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 50) {
            announcement = "[💎] - [🏆: " + stats[Ss.WI] +"]  Platinum · ";
            chatColor = 0xE5D4C1;
        } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 30) {
            announcement = "[🌕] - [🏆: " + stats[Ss.WI] +"]  Gold · ";
            chatColor = 0xCD7F32;
        } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 15) {
            announcement = "[🗿] - [🏆: " + stats[Ss.WI] +"]  Silver · ";
            chatColor = 0xCD7F32;
        } else if (stats[Ss.WI] >= 5) {
            announcement = "[🍂] - [🏆: " + stats[Ss.WI] +"]  Bronze · ";
            chatColor = 0xCD7F32;
        } else {
            announcement = "💫 Principiante · ";
            chatColor = 0xFFFFFF;
        }
        
        announcement += player.name + ": " + originalMessage;
        room.sendAnnouncement(announcement, null, chatColor);
        return false;
    }
    else {
        room.sendAnnouncement("❌ " + player.name + ": " + originalMessage, null, 0xABAEA7);
        return false;
    }
}

room.onPlayerActivity = function(player) {
    setActivity(player, 0);
}

room.onPlayerBallKick = function(player) {
    if (lastPlayersTouched[0] == null || player.id != lastPlayersTouched[0].id) {
        !activePlay ? activePlay = true : null;
        lastTeamTouched = player.team;
        lastPlayersTouched[1] = lastPlayersTouched[0];
        lastPlayersTouched[0] = player;
    }
}

/* GESTÃO DE JOGO */

room.onGameStart = function(byPlayer) {
    // Asignar uniformes automáticos inmediatamente cuando inicia la partida
    try {
        assignRandomUniforms();
    } catch(e) {}
    
    // PAUSAR cambio automático de uniformes durante la partida
    stopAutoUniformInterval();
    
    game = new Game(Date.now(), room.getScores(), []);
    countAFK = true;
    activePlay = false;
    goldenGoal = false;
    endGameVariable = false;
    lastPlayersTouched = [null, null];
    Rposs = 0;
    Bposs = 0;
    GKList = [];
    allReds = [];
    allBlues = [];
    
    // Limpiar marcas de skip al iniciar partida (!skip solo válido por session)
    try {
        Object.keys(localStorage).forEach(function(key) {
            if (key.startsWith('skip_')) {
                localStorage.removeItem(key);
            }
        });
    } catch (e) {}
    
    room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("🥅 PARTIDA INICIANDO 🥅"), null, Cor.White, "bold");
    room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("✨ UNIFORMES ASIGNADOS AUTOMÁTICAMENTE ✨"), null, 0xFDC43A, "bold");
    room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("Si quieres, puedes cambiar con !unis para personalizar 😎"), null, 0x2EF55D, "bold");
    // mostrar los uniformes asignados recién generados justo aquí
    room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("🏁 Uniformes asignados: 🔴 " + currentUniforms.red + " - 🔵 " + currentUniforms.blue), null, 0xFDC43A, "bold");
    room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("¿Eres nuevo? usa el comando /extrapolation 100 Te ayudara a ganar esta partida!!!"), null, 0xFFAE00, "bold");
    room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("[💬] Utilice 't' mensaje para hablar solo con tu equipo!"), null, 0x5EE7FF, "bold");
    
    // Iniciar ventana de apuestas (se muestra al final)
    startBettingWindow();
    
    if (TeamR.length == maxTeamSize && TeamB.length == maxTeamSize) {
        for (var i = 0; i < maxTeamSize; i++) {
            allReds.push(TeamR[i]);
            allBlues.push(TeamB[i]);
        }
    }
    for (var i = 0; i < extendedP.length; i++) {
        extendedP[i][eP.GK] = 0;
        extendedP[i][eP.ACT] = 0;
        room.getPlayer(extendedP[i][eP.ID]) == null ? extendedP.splice(i, 1) : null;
    }
    deactivateChooseMode();
}

room.onGameStop = function(byPlayer) {
    // NO reiniciar el intervalo automático - evita cambios en menu de selección y pausas
    // Los uniformes solo se asignan al iniciar partida, no cambian después
    
    if (byPlayer.id == 0 && endGameVariable) {
        updateTeams();
        if (inChooseMode) {
            if (players.length == 2 * maxTeamSize) {
                inChooseMode = false;
                resetBtn();
                for (var i = 0; i < maxTeamSize; i++) {
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        randomBtn();
                    }, 400 * i);
                }
                setTimeout(() => {
                    room.startGame();
                }, 2000);
            } else {
                if (lastWinner == Team.RED) {
                    blueToSpecBtn();
                } else if (lastWinner == Team.BLUE) {
                    redToSpecBtn();
                    blueToRedBtn();
                } else {
                    resetBtn();
                }
                setTimeout(() => {
                    topBtn();
                }, 500);
            }
        } else {
            if (players.length == 2) {
                if (lastWinner == Team.BLUE) {
                    room.setPlayerTeam(TeamB[0].id, Team.RED);
                    room.setPlayerTeam(TeamR[0].id, Team.BLUE);
                }
                setTimeout(() => {
                    room.startGame();
                }, 2000);
            } else if (players.length == 3 || players.length >= 2 * maxTeamSize + 1) {
                if (lastWinner == Team.RED) {
                    blueToSpecBtn();
                } else {
                    redToSpecBtn();
                    blueToRedBtn();
                }
                setTimeout(() => {
                    topBtn();
                }, 200);
                setTimeout(() => {
                    room.startGame();
                }, 2000);
            } else if (players.length == 4) {
                resetBtn();
                setTimeout(() => {
                    randomBtn();
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        randomBtn();
                    }, 500);
                }, 500);
                setTimeout(() => {
                    room.startGame();
                }, 2000);
            } else if (players.length == 5 || players.length >= 2 * maxTeamSize + 1) {
                if (lastWinner == Team.RED) {
                    blueToSpecBtn();
                } else {
                    redToSpecBtn();
                    blueToRedBtn();
                }
                setTimeout(() => {
                    topBtn();
                }, 200);
                activateChooseMode();
            } else if (players.length == 6) {
                resetBtn();
                setTimeout(() => {
                    randomBtn();
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        randomBtn();
                        setTimeout(() => {
                            randomBtn();
                        }, 500);
                    }, 500);
                }, 500);
                setTimeout(() => {
                    room.startGame();
                }, 2000);
            }
        }
    }
}

room.onGamePause = function(byPlayer) {}

room.onGameUnpause = function(byPlayer) {
    if (TeamR.length == 4 && TeamB.length == 4 && inChooseMode || (TeamR.length == TeamB.length && teamS.length < 2 && inChooseMode)) {
        deactivateChooseMode();
    }
}

room.onTeamGoal = function(team) {
    teamgoaler = team;
    let assistencia = "";
    let goleador = "";
    let goalMaker = lastPlayersTouched[0].id;
    activePlay = false;
    countAFK = false;
    const scores = room.getScores();
    game.scores = scores;
    if (lastPlayersTouched[0] != null && lastPlayersTouched[0].team == team) {
		if (lastPlayersTouched[1] != null && lastPlayersTouched[1].team == team) {
			var frasegol = frasesGols[(Math.random() * frasesGols.length) | 0]
			var fraseasis = frasesasis[(Math.random() * frasesasis.length) | 0]
		//	room.sendAnnouncement("⚽👥 " + getTime(scores) + frasegol + lastPlayersTouched[0].name + fraseasis + lastPlayersTouched[1].name + " | Velocidade do chute: " + ballSpeed.toPrecision(4).toString() + "km/h " + (team == Team.RED ? "🔴" : "🔵"),null,(team == Team.RED ? Cor.Vermelho : 0x5ec0f1),'bold');
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("TOCA LA MÚÚSICAAA, ES GOOOOOL!!!"), null, Cor.Verde, "bold");
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("         ⚽ Gol de " + lastPlayersTouched[0].name + " ⚽"), null, Cor.White, "bold");
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("👟 Asistencia: " + lastPlayersTouched[1].name + " 👟"), null, Cor.White, "bold");
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("Velocidad de chute: " + ballSpeed.toPrecision(4).toString() + " km/h"), null, Cor.White, "normal");
        game.goals.push(new Goal(scores.time, team, lastPlayersTouched[0], lastPlayersTouched[1]));
        // Dar coins al goleador SOLO si hay 8 jugadores
        try {
            updateTeams();
            if (players.length >= 2 * maxTeamSize) {
                var golerAuth = getAuth(lastPlayersTouched[0]);
                if (golerAuth) {
                    var goleadorStats = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(golerAuth)) || [0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", 0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", "player", lastPlayersTouched[0].name, 0, null, 0, "", 0];
                    var goalCoinsAmount = getGoalCoins(goleadorStats);
                    goleadorStats[Ss.CO] = (Number(goleadorStats[Ss.CO]) || 0) + goalCoinsAmount;
                    localStorage.setItem(golerAuth, JSON.stringify(goleadorStats));
                    room.sendAnnouncement("💰 " + lastPlayersTouched[0].name + " + " + goalCoinsAmount + " Coin" + (goalCoinsAmount > 1 ? "s" : "") + "!", null, 0xFFD700, "normal");
                }
            }
        } catch(e) {}
        }
        else {
            var frasegol = frasesGols[(Math.random() * frasesGols.length) | 0]
        ///	room.sendAnnouncement("⚽ " + getTime(scores) + frasegol + lastPlayersTouched[0].name + "! | Velocidade do chute: " + ballSpeed.toPrecision(4).toString() + "km/h " + (team == Team.RED ? "🔴" : "🔵"),null,(team == Team.RED ? Cor.Vermelho : 0x5ec0f1),'bold');
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("TOCA LA MÚÚSICAAA, ES GOOOOOL!!!"), null, Cor.Verde, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("         ⚽ Gol de " + lastPlayersTouched[0].name + " ⚽"), null, Cor.White, "bold");
            room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("Velocidad de chute: " + ballSpeed.toPrecision(4).toString() + " km/h"), null, Cor.White, "normal");
            game.goals.push(new Goal(scores.time, team, lastPlayersTouched[0], null));
            // Dar coins al goleador SOLO si hay 8 jugadores
            try {
                updateTeams();
                if (players.length >= 2 * maxTeamSize) {
                    var golerAuth = getAuth(lastPlayersTouched[0]);
                    if (golerAuth) {
                        var goleadorStats = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(golerAuth)) || [0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", 0, 0, 0, 0, "0.00", "player", lastPlayersTouched[0].name, 0, null, 0, "", 0];
                        var goalCoinsAmount = getGoalCoins(goleadorStats);
                        goleadorStats[Ss.CO] = (Number(goleadorStats[Ss.CO]) || 0) + goalCoinsAmount;
                        localStorage.setItem(golerAuth, JSON.stringify(goleadorStats));
                        room.sendAnnouncement("💰 " + lastPlayersTouched[0].name + " + " + goalCoinsAmount + " Coin" + (goalCoinsAmount > 1 ? "s" : "") + "!", null, 0xFFD700, "normal");
                    }
                }
            } catch(e) {}
        }
			setTimeout(function () {
				room.setPlayerAvatar(goalMaker, "🎯")
				setTimeout(function () {
					room.setPlayerAvatar(goalMaker, "⚽")
					setTimeout(function () {
						room.setPlayerAvatar(goalMaker, null)
					}, 3000);
				}, 1200);
			}, 1);
		
			if (lastPlayersTouched[1] != null && lastPlayersTouched[1].team == team) {
				let goalAssist = lastPlayersTouched[1].id;
				assistencia = lastPlayersTouched[1];
				setTimeout(function () {
					room.setPlayerAvatar(goalAssist, "🤝")
					setTimeout(function () {
						room.setPlayerAvatar(goalAssist, "👟")
						setTimeout(function () {
							room.setPlayerAvatar(goalAssist, null)
						}, 2500);
					}, 1000);
				}, 1);
			}

		}
		else {
			var fraseautogol = frasesautogol[(Math.random() * frasesautogol.length) | 0]
		//	room.sendAnnouncement("🤡 " + getTime(scores) + fraseautogol + lastPlayersTouched[0].name + "! | Velocidade do chute: " + ballSpeed.toPrecision(4).toString() + "km/h " + (team == Team.RED ? "🔴" : "🔵"),null,(team == Team.RED ? Cor.Vermelho : 0x5ec0f1),'bold');
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("🤦‍♂️ ES GOL EN CONTRA!! 🤦‍♂️"), null, Cor.Yellow, "bold");
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("🤡 Gol de " + lastPlayersTouched[0].name + " 🤡"), null, Cor.White, "bold");
        room.sendAnnouncement(centerText("Velocidad de chute: " + ballSpeed.toPrecision(4).toString() + " km/h"), null, Cor.White, "normal");
        game.goals.push(new Goal(scores.time, team, null, null));
			setTimeout(function () {
				room.setPlayerAvatar(goalMaker, "🤦‍♂️")
				setTimeout(function () {
					room.setPlayerAvatar(goalMaker, "🤡")
					setTimeout(function () {
						room.setPlayerAvatar(goalMaker, null)
					}, 3000);
				}, 1000);
			}, 1);

        golcontra(lastPlayersTouched[0]);
		}
		
		if (scores.scoreLimit != 0 && (scores.red == scores.scoreLimit || scores.blue == scores.scoreLimit && scores.blue > 0 || goldenGoal == true)) {
			endGame(team);
			goldenGoal = false;
			setTimeout(() => { room.stopGame(); }, 1000);
		}
	}

room.onPositionsReset = function() {
    countAFK = true;
    lastPlayersTouched = [null, null];
}

/* DIVERSOS */

room.onRoomLink = function(url) {
    // Iniciar temporizador de Top 3 (cada 20 minutos)
    startTop3Timer();
    // Enviar notificación de arranque del servidor a Discord
    sendServerStartupNotification();
}

room.onPlayerAdminChange = function(changedPlayer, byPlayer) {
    if (getMute(changedPlayer) && changedPlayer.admin) {
        room.sendChat(changedPlayer.name + " Fue muteado.");
        setMute(changedPlayer, false);
    }
    if (byPlayer.id != 0 && localStorage.getItem(getAuth(byPlayer)) && JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(getAuth(byPlayer)))[Ss.RL] == "admin") {
        room.sendChat("No se le permite nombrar a un jugador como administrador!", byPlayer.id);
        room.setPlayerAdmin(changedPlayer.id, false);
    }
    // Actualizar rol persistente en localStorage cuando cambie admin
    try {
        var stats;
        if (localStorage.getItem(getAuth(changedPlayer))) {
            stats = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(getAuth(changedPlayer)));
        } else {
            stats = [0,0,0,0,"0.00",0,0,0,0,"0.00","player"];
        }
        stats[Ss.RL] = changedPlayer.admin ? "admin" : "player";
        localStorage.setItem(getAuth(changedPlayer), JSON.stringify(stats));
    } catch (e) {}
}

room.onStadiumChange = function(newStadiumName, byPlayer) {
    // TOP 3 se muestra automáticamente cada 20 minutos (no al cambiar estadio)
};

room.onGameTick = function() {
    checkTime();
    getLastTouchOfTheBall();
    getStats();
    handleInactivity();
}
